*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_goodsmvt_create .
  DATA : lt_field  LIKE TABLE OF sval WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_return.


  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_mov-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_mov.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_mov-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_mov INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  _cls lt_field.
  "출고일자
  lt_field-tabname = 'MSEG'.
  lt_field-fieldname = 'BUDAT_MKPF'.
  lt_field-fieldtext = 'MVT Date'.
  lt_field-value = ''.
  APPEND lt_field. CLEAR lt_field.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      popup_title     = 'MVT Date'
      start_column    = '50'
      start_row       = '10'
    IMPORTING
      returncode      = lv_return
    TABLES
      fields          = lt_field
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_fields = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR lv_return = 'A' OR lt_field-value IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s008.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.


  CLEAR gv_budat.
  READ TABLE lt_field INDEX 1.
  gv_budat = lt_field-value.

  CHECK gv_budat IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR gv_mblnr.
  CLEAR gs_goodsmvt_header.
  gs_goodsmvt_header-pstng_date = gv_budat.
  gs_goodsmvt_header-doc_date   = gv_budat.

  _cls gt_goodsmvt_item.
  LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

    "2021.06.09 추가
    "이동일자가 유효일자보다 작으면 에러처리
    IF gt_mov-xchpf EQ gc_x AND gt_mov-vfdat < gv_budat.
      gt_mov-icon = gc_err.
      gt_mov-msg = TEXT-e09.
      MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING icon msg.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    _cls gt_goodsmvt_item.

    gt_goodsmvt_item-reserv_no    = gt_mov-rsnum.
    gt_goodsmvt_item-res_item     = gt_mov-rspos.

    gt_goodsmvt_item-material     =  gt_mov-matnr.
    gt_goodsmvt_item-plant        =  gt_mov-werks.
    gt_goodsmvt_item-item_text    =  gt_mov-sgtxt.

    IF gt_mov-charg IS NOT INITIAL.
      gt_goodsmvt_item-batch = gt_mov-charg.
    ENDIF.

    gt_goodsmvt_item-entry_qnt = gt_mov-giqty.
    gt_goodsmvt_item-entry_uom = gt_mov-meins. "2021.06.08 단위추가
    gt_goodsmvt_item-move_type        = gt_mov-bwart.
    _app gt_goodsmvt_item .


    CLEAR: gv_mblnr, gv_mjahr.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE'
      EXPORTING
        goodsmvt_header  = gs_goodsmvt_header
        goodsmvt_code    = '04'
      IMPORTING
        materialdocument = gv_mblnr
        matdocumentyear  = gv_mjahr
      TABLES
        goodsmvt_item    = gt_goodsmvt_item
        return           = gt_return.

    CLEAR gt_mov.
    IF gv_mblnr IS INITIAL.

      gt_mov-icon = gc_err.

      IF gt_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        gt_mov-msg = gt_return[ type = gc_e ]-message.
      ENDIF.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
    ELSE.


      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
        EXPORTING
          wait = gc_x.
      gt_mov-icon = gc_suc.
      gt_mov-mblnr = gv_mblnr.
      gt_mov-mjahr = gv_mjahr.
      CONCATENATE TEXT-m07  gv_mblnr INTO gt_mov-msg
                                       SEPARATED BY space.



    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING mblnr mjahr icon msg.

  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

FORM grid_set_fieldcatalog5 .

* SLIS로 만든 필드 구조를 CLASS ALV 구조로 생성

             WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.

*Field Catalog Append
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ICON' TEXT-f09.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'MSG' TEXT-f10.

  LOOP AT gt_fcat ASSIGNING <ls_fcat>.
    CASE <ls_fcat>-fieldname.
      WHEN 'ICON'.
        <ls_fcat>-col_pos = 0.
      WHEN 'MBLNR'.
*        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재문서'.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_GOODSMVT_CANCEL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text

*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_goodsmvt_cancel .
  DATA lv_matdoc    TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-mat_doc.
  DATA lv_mjahr     TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-doc_year.
  DATA lv_budat     TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-pstng_date.
  DATA lt_item      TYPE TABLE OF bapi2017_gm_item_04.
  DATA ls_item      TYPE bapi2017_gm_item_04.

  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_dis-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_dis TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_dis.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_dis-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_dis INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_dis WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
    _cls lt_item.
    lv_matdoc = gt_dis-mblnr.
    lv_mjahr = gt_dis-mjahr.
    lv_budat = gt_dis-budat.

    ls_item-matdoc_item = gt_dis-zeile - 1.    "2021.05.21 추가
    APPEND ls_item TO lt_item.

    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_GOODSMVT_CANCEL'
      EXPORTING
        materialdocument    = lv_matdoc
        matdocumentyear     = lv_mjahr
        goodsmvt_pstng_date = lv_budat
      TABLES
        return              = gt_return
        goodsmvt_matdocitem = lt_item.

    READ TABLE gt_return WITH KEY type = gc_e.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gt_dis-icon = gc_err.

      IF gt_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        gt_dis-msg = gt_return[ type = gc_e ]-message.
      ENDIF.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
    ELSE.

      gt_dis-icon = gc_suc.
      gt_dis-msg = TEXT-m03.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
        EXPORTING
          wait = gc_x.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_dis TRANSPORTING icon msg.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

**          RS~ERFMG - RS~ENMNG AS ERFMG, "출고수량  "2021.04.02 요청수량처럼 ENMNG차감
           rs~erfmg AS bdmng,
*           RS~ENMNG, "2021.07.14   mseg-erfmg로 발췌,별도로직구현
           rs~erfmg - rs~enmng AS erfmg,
"END
          rs~erfme, "출고단위
          ma~xchpf,

          rs~bwart, "2021.03.30 추가
          rs~postp "2021.03.30 추가
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @gt_inp

                              ON cv~aufnr EQ ap~aufnr
                     LEFT JOIN mara AS ma
*                             ON CV~PLNBEZ EQ MA~MATNR
                             ON rs~matnr EQ ma~matnr

                              ON cv~plnbez EQ m1~matnr

     AND ap~charg IN @s_charg
     AND rs~xloek EQ @space
     AND rs~bwart EQ @gc_261 "2021.04.05 추가
     AND rs~kzear EQ @space"2021.04.05 추가
     AND rs~xwaok EQ @gc_x "2021.04.05 추가
     AND cv~gstrp IN @s_gstrp "2021.05.18 추가
     AND rs~rgekz EQ ''        "2021.05.24 추가
     AND rs~matnr IN @s_idnrk.  "2021.05.24 추가

  _cls lt_ent.
  lt_ent[] = VALUE #( BASE lt_ent[] FOR ls_inp IN gt_inp
                        ( CORRESPONDING #( ls_inp ) ) ).

  SORT lt_ent BY werks matnr lgort.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ent COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

  IF lt_ent[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    "2021.06.08 - 조건에 상관없이 저장위치 재고로 발췌
    "배치관리 안하는 자재의 재고 발췌 (MARD)
    SELECT DISTINCT

                                 ON md~matnr EQ ma~matnr

                                AND mt~spras EQ @sy-langu
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_ent
     WHERE md~werks EQ @lt_ent-werks
       AND md~matnr EQ @lt_ent-matnr
       AND md~lgort EQ @lt_ent-lgort
*       AND MA~XCHPF EQ @SPACE "2021.06.08 주석
       AND md~labst NE 0.
    SORT lt_mard BY werks matnr lgort.




    "2021.06.08 주석
    "BEGIN
*    "배치관리 하는 자재의 배치재고 발췌 (MCHB)
*    SELECT DISTINCT
*            MH~WERKS,
*            MH~MATNR,

*                                 ON MH~MATNR EQ MA~MATNR
*                        INNER JOIN MAKT AS MT

*                                AND MT~SPRAS EQ @SY-LANGU

*       AND MH~MATNR EQ @LT_ENT-MATNR

*       AND MA~XCHPF EQ @GC_X

*    SORT LT_MCHB BY WERKS MATNR LGORT CHARG.
    "END
  ENDIF.


  "출고저장위치 재고
  LOOP AT gt_inp ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_inp>).
*    IF <LS_INP>-XCHPF EQ SPACE. "2021.06.08 주석
    READ TABLE lt_mard INTO DATA(ls_mard) WITH KEY werks = <ls_inp>-werks
                                                       matnr = <ls_inp>-matnr
                                                       lgort = <ls_inp>-lgort
                                                       BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      IF ls_mard-meins EQ 'ROL'.

        CALL FUNCTION 'MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT'
          EXPORTING

          IMPORTING
            e_menge  = ls_mard-labst.

      ENDIF.



      <ls_inp>-labst = ls_mard-labst.
    ENDIF.
*    ELSE. "2021.06.08 주석
*      READ TABLE LT_MCHB INTO DATA(LS_MCHB) WITH KEY WERKS = <LS_INP>-WERKS "2021.06.08 주석
*                                                        MATNR = <LS_INP>-MATNR "2021.06.08 주석
*                                                        LGORT = <LS_INP>-LGORT "2021.06.08 주석

*    "이미 311로 처리된자재배치 값 디폴트 발췌
*    DATA(LV_STR) = <LS_INP>-RSNUM && <LS_INP>-RSPOS.

*      FROM RESB

    IF <ls_inp>-zlot IS NOT INITIAL.

      "2021.05.18 구성부품의 배치에 대한 유효기간 필드 추가(MCH1-VFDAT)
      CLEAR  <ls_inp>-vfdat.
      SELECT SINGLE vfdat INTO <ls_inp>-vfdat
        FROM mch1
       WHERE matnr EQ <ls_inp>-matnr
         AND charg EQ <ls_inp>-zlot
         AND lvorm EQ space.

      "2021.05.18 검사로트번호 발췌
*자재코드/배치번호를 Key로 QALS의 테이블을 조회한다.(ART는 0110(외주검사), 04(생산입고검사), 05(무상입고검사)에 대한 전부 리스트 가져온다.)
*기존으로 조회한다음 QALS-OBJNR의 값을 기준으로 상태 TALBLE JEST를 찾아서 I0224(로트취소)가 아닌 대상의 검사로트 번호를 화면에 보여준다.

      "품질검사로트
      CLEAR <ls_inp>-prueflos.

        INTO <ls_inp>-prueflos
        FROM qals AS qa
       WHERE art IN ( '0110', '04' , '05', '01' )
         AND qa~matnr EQ <ls_inp>-matnr
         AND qa~charg EQ <ls_inp>-zlot
         AND NOT EXISTS
              ( SELECT *
                   FROM jest
                  WHERE objnr EQ qa~objnr
                    AND stat EQ 'I0224'
                    AND inact EQ '' ).

      "2021.05.18 old batch 값 발췌
      CLEAR <ls_inp>-ausp1.
      PERFORM   GET_OLD_BATCH_CLASSFICATION USING <ls_inp>-werks
                                                   <ls_inp>-matnr
                                                   <ls_inp>-zlot
                                            CHANGING <ls_inp>-ausp1.

    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE licha INTO <ls_inp>-licha
      FROM mch1
     WHERE matnr EQ <ls_inp>-plnbez
       AND charg EQ <ls_inp>-charg
       AND lvorm EQ ''.

    "2021.07.13 기본단위/기본수량
    SELECT SINGLE mr~meinh mr~umren
      INTO ( <ls_inp>-b_meinh, <ls_inp>-b_umren )
      FROM mara AS ma INNER JOIN marm AS mr
                                ON ma~meins EQ mr~meinh
     WHERE ma~matnr EQ <ls_inp>-matnr.

    "2021.07.13 대체단위/대체수량
    SELECT SINGLE meinh umren
      INTO ( <ls_inp>-c_meinh, <ls_inp>-c_umren )
      FROM marm
     WHERE matnr EQ <ls_inp>-matnr
       AND meinh NE <ls_inp>-b_meinh.

    "2021.07.14 생산투입완료 수량

    SELECT SINGLE mg~erfmg
      INTO <ls_inp>-enmng
      FROM mseg AS mg
     WHERE mg~rsnum EQ <ls_inp>-rsnum
       AND mg~rspos EQ <ls_inp>-rspos
       AND mg~bwart EQ '261'
       AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT *
                    FROM mseg
                   WHERE smbln EQ mg~mblnr
                     AND sjahr EQ mg~mjahr
                     AND smblp EQ mg~zeile ).
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_inp BY rsnum rspos aufnr gstrp.

  IF gt_inp[] IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s004 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
  ELSE.
    gv_cnt = lines( gt_inp ).
    MESSAGE s001 WITH gv_cnt.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GRID_SET_FIELDCATALOG6
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM grid_set_fieldcatalog6 .
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <ls_fcat> TYPE lvc_s_fcat.
  DATA: lv_date TYPE sydatum.

  lv_date = sy-datum + 1.
  SET PARAMETER ID 'ALVBUFFER' FIELD lv_date.
  CLEAR:   gt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.
  REFRESH: gt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
      i_program_name     = sy-repid
      i_internal_tabname = 'GT_INP'
      i_inclname         = sy-repid
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat        = gt_slis_fcat[].

* SLIS로 만든 필드 구조를 CLASS ALV 구조로 생성

  CALL FUNCTION 'LVC_TRANSFER_FROM_SLIS'
    EXPORTING
      it_fieldcat_alv = gt_slis_fcat
    IMPORTING
      et_fieldcat_lvc = gt_fcat[]
    TABLES
      it_data         = gt_inp
    EXCEPTIONS
      it_data_missing = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF  sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE  ID sy-msgid  TYPE sy-msgty  NUMBER sy-msgno
             WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

*Field Catalog Append
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ICON' '상태'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'MSG' '메세지'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ZSCAN_LABEL' '인식라벨'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ZPRINT_CNT' '인쇄매수'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ZPRINT_SPLIT' '인쇄분할'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'AUSP1' 'Old 배치번호'.

* Field Catalog Modify
  LOOP AT gt_fcat ASSIGNING <ls_fcat>.
    CASE <ls_fcat>-fieldname.
      WHEN 'ICON'.
        <ls_fcat>-col_pos = 0.
      WHEN 'AUFNR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '생산오더'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.
      WHEN 'GSTRP'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '오더 시작일'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'PLNBEZ'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.

      WHEN 'MAKTX'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재내역'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'CHARG'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '배치번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'MATNR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '구성품'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.
      WHEN 'MATNR_T'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '구성품내역'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'LABST'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '출고저장위치 재고'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-qfieldname = 'ERFME'.
      WHEN 'LGORT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '출고저장위치'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-edit = gc_x.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_col_style_total.
        <ls_fcat>-f4availabl = gc_x.
      WHEN 'VFDAT'. "2021.05.18 추가
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '유효기간'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.

        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.

*        <LS_FCAT>-EDIT = GC_X.

        <ls_fcat>-tech = gc_x.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_fcat BY col_pos fieldname.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form CREATE_TOOLBAR_BUTTON6
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> E_OBJECT
*&      --> E_INTERACTIVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_toolbar_button6  USING po_object
                                TYPE REF TO cl_alv_event_toolbar_set
                       pv_interactive.

  DATA: ls_toolbar  TYPE stb_button.
  CLEAR ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   3               TO   ls_toolbar-butn_type.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.

  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZINP'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_move  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '생산투입'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.

  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL2'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.

  "2021.03.29 배치할당 버튼 추가
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZBAT2'         TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_assign      TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '배치할당'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar     TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.


  "2021.05.18 칭량라벨일괄발행
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL3'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨일괄발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.


  "2021.07.06 칭량라벨 분할 발행
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL4'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨분할발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_goodsmvt_create2 .
  DATA : lt_field  LIKE TABLE OF sval WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_return.

  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_inp-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_inp TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_inp.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_inp-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_inp INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  _cls lt_field.
  "출고일자
  lt_field-tabname = 'MSEG'.
  lt_field-fieldname = 'BUDAT_MKPF'.
  lt_field-fieldtext = '출고일자'.
  lt_field-value = ''.
  APPEND lt_field. CLEAR lt_field.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      popup_title     = '출고일자를 입력하세요'
      start_column    = '50'
      start_row       = '10'
    IMPORTING
      returncode      = lv_return
    TABLES
      fields          = lt_field
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_fields = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR lv_return = 'A' OR lt_field-value IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s008.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.


  CLEAR gv_budat.
  READ TABLE lt_field INDEX 1.
  gv_budat = lt_field-value.

  CHECK gv_budat IS NOT INITIAL.

  CLEAR gv_mblnr.
  CLEAR gs_goodsmvt_header.
  gs_goodsmvt_header-pstng_date = gv_budat.
  gs_goodsmvt_header-doc_date   = gv_budat.

  _cls gt_goodsmvt_item.
  LOOP AT gt_inp WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
                    AND mblnr IS INITIAL.
    "2021.05.18 - 유효기간이 출고일보다 크거나 같을경우 또는 유효기간이 없을 경우에만 투입처리
    IF ( gt_inp-xchpf EQ gc_x AND
        gt_inp-vfdat IS NOT INITIAL AND
       gt_inp-vfdat >= gv_budat ) OR
        gt_inp-xchpf EQ space .

      _cls gt_goodsmvt_item.

      gt_goodsmvt_item-reserv_no    = gt_inp-rsnum.
      gt_goodsmvt_item-res_item     = gt_inp-rspos.

*    GT_GOODSMVT_ITEM-MATERIAL     =  GT_INP-MATNR.
*    GT_GOODSMVT_ITEM-PLANT        =  GT_INP-WERKS.
      gt_goodsmvt_item-item_text    =  gt_inp-sgtxt.
      gt_goodsmvt_item-stge_loc     = gt_inp-lgort .

      "2021.04.02 1순위 인식라벨 뒤10자리 , 2순위 기존 배치값
      IF gt_inp-zscan_label IS NOT INITIAL.
        gt_goodsmvt_item-batch = |{ gt_inp-zscan_label+18(10) ALPHA = OUT }|.
      ELSE.
        IF gt_inp-zlot IS NOT INITIAL.
          gt_goodsmvt_item-batch = gt_inp-zlot.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      gt_goodsmvt_item-entry_qnt = gt_inp-erfmg.
      gt_goodsmvt_item-move_type        = gc_261.
      _app gt_goodsmvt_item .


      CLEAR gv_mblnr.
      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE'
        EXPORTING
          goodsmvt_header  = gs_goodsmvt_header
          goodsmvt_code    = '03'
        IMPORTING
          materialdocument = gv_mblnr
        TABLES
          goodsmvt_item    = gt_goodsmvt_item
          return           = gt_return.

      CLEAR gt_inp.
      IF gv_mblnr IS INITIAL.

        gt_inp-icon = gc_err.

        IF gt_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          gt_inp-msg = gt_return[ type = gc_e ]-message.
        ENDIF.

        CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
      ELSE.


        CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
          EXPORTING
            wait = gc_x.
        gt_inp-icon = gc_suc.
        gt_inp-mblnr = gv_mblnr.
        CONCATENATE TEXT-m07  gv_mblnr INTO gt_inp-msg
                                         SEPARATED BY space.

      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      gt_inp-icon = gc_err.
      gt_inp-msg = TEXT-m09.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_inp TRANSPORTING mblnr icon msg.

  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GRID_STYLE_MODIFY2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM grid_style_modify2 .
  CALL METHOD go_grid->set_ready_for_input
    EXPORTING
      i_ready_for_input = 1.     "수정

  CASE gc_x.
    WHEN ra_mov.
      LOOP AT gt_mov.

        PERFORM  MODIFY_EDIT_STYLE2 USING gt_mov.

        CLEAR  gt_mov-handle_style.
        INSERT LINES OF gt_edit
               INTO TABLE gt_mov-handle_style.

        MODIFY gt_mov FROM gt_mov
                        INDEX sy-tabix
                        TRANSPORTING handle_style.
      ENDLOOP.
*    WHEN ra_inp.
*      LOOP AT gt_inp.
*
*        PERFORM modify_edit_style3  USING gt_inp.
*
*        CLEAR  gt_inp-handle_style.
*        INSERT LINES OF gt_edit
*               INTO TABLE gt_inp-handle_style.
*
*        MODIFY gt_inp FROM gt_inp
*                        INDEX sy-tabix
*                        TRANSPORTING handle_style.
*      ENDLOOP.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form MODIFY_EDIT_STYLE2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> GT_MOV
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM modify_edit_style2  USING ps_outtab LIKE gt_mov.

  DATA: lv_eb(50),
        lv_db(50).
  DATA: ls_fcat  TYPE lvc_s_fcat.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_eb>, <fs_db>.
  REFRESH gt_edit.
* Cell Enabled
  lv_eb = 'CL_GUI_ALV_GRID=>MC_STYLE_ENABLED'.
  ASSIGN (lv_eb) TO <fs_eb>.
* Cell Disabled
  lv_db = 'CL_GUI_ALV_GRID=>MC_STYLE_DISABLED'.
  ASSIGN (lv_db) TO <fs_db>.


  IF ps_outtab-xchpf IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'CHARG'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_eb>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'CHARG'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form DATA_CHANGED_1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> ER_DATA_CHANGED
*&      --> E_ONF4
*&      --> E_ONF4_BEFORE
*&      --> E_ONF4_AFTER
*&      --> E_UCOMM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM data_changed_1  USING po_data_changed TYPE REF TO
                                           cl_alv_changed_data_protocol
                           pv_onf4          TYPE char1
                           pv_onf4_before   TYPE char1
                           pv_onf4_after    TYPE char1
                           pv_ucomm         TYPE sy-ucomm.

  DATA: ls_mov LIKE gt_mov.
  DATA: ls_good_cells TYPE lvc_s_modi,
        lt_good_cells TYPE lvc_t_modi.

  MOVE po_data_changed->mt_good_cells TO lt_good_cells.
  CLEAR ls_mov.
  READ TABLE lt_good_cells INTO ls_good_cells INDEX 1.
  CHECK sy-subrc EQ 0.
  CASE ls_good_cells-fieldname.
    WHEN 'CHARG'.
      CHECK ls_good_cells-value IS NOT INITIAL.

      ls_mov-charg = ls_good_cells-value.

      "GRID CELL값 읽어오기
      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->get_cell_value
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id    " Row ID
          i_fieldname = 'WERKS' " Field Name
        IMPORTING
          e_value     = ls_mov-werks.     " Cell Content

      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->get_cell_value
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id    " Row ID
          i_fieldname = 'MATNR' " Field Name
        IMPORTING
          e_value     = ls_mov-matnr.     " Cell Content

      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->get_cell_value
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id    " Row ID
          i_fieldname = 'LGORT' " Field Name
        IMPORTING
          e_value     = ls_mov-lgort.     " Cell Content


      SELECT SINGLE clabs INTO ls_mov-labst
        FROM mchb
       WHERE matnr EQ ls_mov-matnr
         AND werks EQ ls_mov-werks
         AND lgort EQ ls_mov-lgort
         AND charg EQ ls_mov-charg.


      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->modify_cell
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id
          i_fieldname = 'LABST'
          i_value     = ls_mov-labst.

      "2021.06.09 - 유효기간 발췌
      SELECT SINGLE vfdat INTO ls_mov-vfdat
        FROM mch1
       WHERE matnr EQ ls_mov-matnr
         AND charg EQ ls_mov-charg
         AND lvorm EQ space.

      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->modify_cell
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id
          i_fieldname = 'VFDAT'
          i_value     = ls_mov-vfdat.

    WHEN 'LGORT'.
      CHECK ls_good_cells-value IS NOT INITIAL.

      ls_mov-lgort = ls_good_cells-value.

      "GRID CELL값 읽어오기
      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->get_cell_value
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id    " Row ID
          i_fieldname = 'WERKS' " Field Name
        IMPORTING
          e_value     = ls_mov-werks.     " Cell Content

      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->get_cell_value
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id    " Row ID
          i_fieldname = 'MATNR' " Field Name
        IMPORTING
          e_value     = ls_mov-matnr.     " Cell Content

      SELECT SINGLE labst INTO ls_mov-labst
        FROM mard
       WHERE matnr EQ ls_mov-matnr
         AND werks EQ ls_mov-werks
         AND lgort EQ ls_mov-lgort.


      CALL METHOD po_data_changed->modify_cell
        EXPORTING
          i_row_id    = ls_good_cells-row_id
          i_fieldname = 'LABST'
          i_value     = ls_mov-labst.

  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form MODIFY_EDIT_STYLE3
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> GT_INP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM modify_edit_style3 USING ps_outtab LIKE gt_inp.

  DATA: lv_eb(50),
        lv_db(50).
  DATA: ls_fcat  TYPE lvc_s_fcat.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_eb>, <fs_db>.
  REFRESH gt_edit.
* Cell Enabled
  lv_eb = 'CL_GUI_ALV_GRID=>MC_STYLE_ENABLED'.
  ASSIGN (lv_eb) TO <fs_eb>.
* Cell Disabled
  lv_db = 'CL_GUI_ALV_GRID=>MC_STYLE_DISABLED'.
  ASSIGN (lv_db) TO <fs_db>.

  IF ps_outtab-bwart NE '261' AND
     ps_outtab-postp EQ 'T'.
    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'LGORT'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.

    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'ZLOT'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.

    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'ERFMG'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.

    CLEAR gs_edit.
    gs_edit-fieldname = 'SGTXT'.
    gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
    INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.
  ELSE.

    IF ps_outtab-xchpf IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR gs_edit.
      gs_edit-fieldname = 'ZLOT'.
      gs_edit-style     = <fs_eb>.
      INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR gs_edit.
      gs_edit-fieldname = 'ZLOT'.
      gs_edit-style     = <fs_db>.
      INSERT gs_edit INTO TABLE gt_edit.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form DATA_CHANGED_FINISHED
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> E_MODIFIED
*&      --> ET_GOOD_CELLS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM data_changed_finished  USING pe_modified
                                    pe_endmethod TYPE lvc_t_modi.

  DATA ls_endmethod TYPE LINE OF lvc_t_modi.
  DATA l_stbl TYPE lvc_s_stbl.
  DATA ls_inp LIKE LINE OF gt_inp.
  DATA lv_cnt TYPE i.

  READ TABLE pe_endmethod INTO ls_endmethod INDEX 1.

  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  CLEAR ls_inp.
  READ TABLE gt_inp INTO ls_inp INDEX ls_endmethod-row_id.
  CHECK ls_inp IS NOT INITIAL.

  CASE ls_endmethod-fieldname.
    WHEN 'ZLOT' OR 'LGORT'. "출고저장위치와 배치값 변경 시

      "2021.06.01 - 출고저장위치 또는 배치값 변경시 유효기간/OLD BATCH번호/재고 발췌

*      CHECK LS_INP-ZLOT IS NOT INITIAL AND
*             LS_INP-LGORT IS NOT INITIAL.
*

*      SELECT SINGLE CLABS INTO LS_INP-LABST
*        FROM MCHB
*       WHERE MATNR EQ LS_INP-MATNR
*         AND WERKS EQ LS_INP-WERKS
*         AND LGORT EQ LS_INP-LGORT
*         AND CHARG EQ LS_INP-ZLOT.
*
*      MODIFY GT_INP FROM LS_INP INDEX LS_ENDMETHOD-ROW_ID
*                                  TRANSPORTING LABST.

      SELECT SINGLE labst INTO ls_inp-labst
        FROM mard
       WHERE matnr EQ ls_inp-matnr
        AND werks EQ ls_inp-werks
        AND lgort EQ ls_inp-lgort.



      "2021.05.18 구성부품의 배치에 대한 유효기간 필드 추가(MCH1-VFDAT)
      SELECT SINGLE vfdat INTO ls_inp-vfdat
        FROM mch1
       WHERE matnr EQ ls_inp-matnr
         AND charg EQ ls_inp-zlot
         AND lvorm EQ space.

      "2021.05.18 검사로트번호 발췌
*자재코드/배치번호를 Key로 QALS의 테이블을 조회한다.(ART는 0110(외주검사), 04(생산입고검사), 05(무상입고검사)에 대한 전부 리스트 가져온다.)
*기존으로 조회한다음 QALS-OBJNR의 값을 기준으로 상태 TALBLE JEST를 찾아서 I0224(로트취소)가 아닌 대상의 검사로트 번호를 화면에 보여준다.

      "품질검사로트
      SELECT SINGLE qa~prueflos
        INTO ls_inp-prueflos
        FROM qals AS qa
       WHERE art IN ( '0110', '04' , '05', '01' )
         AND qa~matnr EQ ls_inp-matnr
         AND qa~charg EQ ls_inp-zlot
         AND NOT EXISTS
              ( SELECT *
                   FROM jest
                  WHERE objnr EQ qa~objnr
                    AND stat EQ 'I0224'
                    AND inact EQ '' ).


      "2021.05.18 old batch 값 발췌

      PERFORM   GET_OLD_BATCH_CLASSFICATION USING ls_inp-werks
                                                   ls_inp-matnr
                                                   ls_inp-zlot
                                            CHANGING ls_inp-ausp1.


      MODIFY gt_inp FROM ls_inp INDEX ls_endmethod-row_id
                                  TRANSPORTING labst vfdat prueflos ausp1.

      l_stbl-row = gc_x.
      l_stbl-col = gc_x.
      CALL METHOD go_grid->refresh_table_display
        EXPORTING
          is_stable      = l_stbl
          i_soft_refresh = gc_x.

  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GET_DATA6
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_data6 .

  _cls gt_inpc.
  SELECT DISTINCT
          mg~mblnr, "자재문서
          mg~mjahr, "연도
          mg~zeile, "항번
          mg~werks, "플랜트
          mg~lgort, "저장위치
          ap~charg, "배치
          am~aufnr, "생산오더
          ap~matnr, "자재번호
          mt~maktx, "자재내역
          am~bwart, "이동유형
          am~budat, "처리일자
          am~charg AS zlot, "배치
          rs~matnr AS idnrk , "구성품
          m1~maktx AS ojtxb, "구성품내역
          am~menge, "출고수량
*          AM~ERFME, "단위
          ma~meins AS erfme, "단위  "2021.07.13
          mg~sgtxt,
          rs~bdmng "2021.06.21 요청수량
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @gt_inpc
    FROM mseg AS mg INNER JOIN aufm AS am
                              ON mg~mblnr EQ am~mblnr
                             AND mg~mjahr EQ am~mjahr
                             AND mg~zeile EQ am~zeile
                      INNER JOIN afpo AS ap
                               ON am~aufnr EQ ap~aufnr
                      INNER JOIN resb AS rs
                              ON am~rsnum EQ rs~rsnum
                             AND am~rspos EQ rs~rspos
                      INNER JOIN mara AS ma
                               ON rs~matnr EQ ma~matnr  "2021.07.13
                      LEFT JOIN makt AS mt
                          ON ap~matnr EQ mt~matnr
                         AND mt~spras EQ @sy-langu
                      LEFT JOIN makt AS m1
                          ON rs~matnr EQ m1~matnr
                         AND m1~spras EQ @sy-langu
   WHERE mg~werks EQ @p_werks
     AND ap~matnr IN @s_matnr
     AND am~aufnr IN @s_aufnr
     AND am~charg IN @s_charg
     AND mg~bwart EQ '261'
     AND mg~budat_mkpf IN @s_gidat
     AND rs~matnr IN @s_idnrk  "2021.05.24 추가
         AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT *
                          FROM mseg
                         WHERE smbln EQ mg~mblnr
                           AND sjahr EQ mg~mjahr
                           AND smblp EQ mg~zeile ).

  SORT gt_inpc BY mblnr mjahr zeile aufnr.


  "2021.05.21 - 생산투입에 추가한 로직 동일하게 적용
  "BEGIN
  LOOP AT gt_inpc  WHERE zlot IS NOT INITIAL.

    "2021.05.18 구성부품의 배치에 대한 유효기간 필드 추가(MCH1-VFDAT)
    CLEAR gt_inpc-vfdat.
    SELECT SINGLE vfdat INTO gt_inpc-vfdat
      FROM mch1
     WHERE matnr EQ gt_inpc-idnrk
       AND charg EQ gt_inpc-zlot
       AND lvorm EQ space.

    "2021.05.18 검사로트번호 발췌
*자재코드/배치번호를 Key로 QALS의 테이블을 조회한다.(ART는 0110(외주검사), 04(생산입고검사), 05(무상입고검사)에 대한 전부 리스트 가져온다.)
*기존으로 조회한다음 QALS-OBJNR의 값을 기준으로 상태 TALBLE JEST를 찾아서 I0224(로트취소)가 아닌 대상의 검사로트 번호를 화면에 보여준다.

    "품질검사로트
    CLEAR gt_inpc-prueflos.
    SELECT SINGLE qa~prueflos
      INTO gt_inpc-prueflos
      FROM qals AS qa
     WHERE art IN ( '0110', '04' , '05', '01' )
       AND qa~matnr EQ gt_inpc-idnrk
       AND qa~charg EQ gt_inpc-zlot
       AND NOT EXISTS
            ( SELECT *
                 FROM jest
                WHERE objnr EQ qa~objnr
                  AND stat EQ 'I0224'
                  AND inact EQ '' ).


    "2021.05.18 old batch 값 발췌

    CLEAR gt_inpc-ausp1.
    PERFORM   GET_OLD_BATCH_CLASSFICATION USING gt_inpc-werks
                                                 gt_inpc-idnrk
                                                 gt_inpc-zlot
                                          CHANGING gt_inpc-ausp1.



    MODIFY gt_inpc.
  ENDLOOP.
  "END



  IF gt_inpc[] IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s004 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
  ELSE.
    gv_cnt = lines( gt_inpc ).
    MESSAGE s001 WITH gv_cnt.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

*& Form DEFINE_GRID_DISPLAY6

FORM define_grid_display6 .
  DATA: lt_excl_func  TYPE ui_functions,
        lt_excl_func2 TYPE ui_functions,
        ls_layo       TYPE lvc_s_layo,
        ls_layo2      TYPE lvc_s_layo.

  IF go_grid IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT go_grid
      EXPORTING
        i_parent = go_docking_container.

*    PERFORM GRID_SET_SORT6         TABLES GT_SORT_GRID.
    PERFORM  GRID_SET_TOOLBAR CHANGING gt_excl_func .
    PERFORM  GRID_SET_LAYO CHANGING gs_layout_alv .
    PERFORM  GRID_SET_FIELDCATALOG7.

    PERFORM  GRID_EVENT_HANDLER_REGISTER USING go_grid 'OBJ7'.

    PERFORM  GRID_DISPLAY2.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GRID_SET_FIELDCATALOG7
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM grid_set_fieldcatalog7 .
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <ls_fcat> TYPE lvc_s_fcat.
  DATA: lv_date TYPE sydatum.

  lv_date = sy-datum + 1.
  SET PARAMETER ID 'ALVBUFFER' FIELD lv_date.
  CLEAR:   gt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.
  REFRESH: gt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
      i_program_name     = sy-repid
      i_internal_tabname = 'GT_INPC'
      i_inclname         = sy-repid
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat        = gt_slis_fcat[].

* SLIS로 만든 필드 구조를 CLASS ALV 구조로 생성

  CALL FUNCTION 'LVC_TRANSFER_FROM_SLIS'
    EXPORTING
      it_fieldcat_alv = gt_slis_fcat
    IMPORTING
      et_fieldcat_lvc = gt_fcat[]
    TABLES
      it_data         = gt_inpc
    EXCEPTIONS
      it_data_missing = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF  sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE  ID sy-msgid  TYPE sy-msgty  NUMBER sy-msgno
             WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

*Field Catalog Append
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'ICON' '상태'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'MSG' '메세지'.
  PERFORM  APPEND_CATALOG USING gt_fcat 'AUSP1' 'Old 배치번호'.

* Field Catalog Modify
  LOOP AT gt_fcat ASSIGNING <ls_fcat>.
    CASE <ls_fcat>-fieldname.
      WHEN 'ICON'.
        <ls_fcat>-col_pos = 0.
      WHEN 'MBLNR_C'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '취소자재문서'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.
      WHEN 'MJAHR_C'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '취소연도'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.

      WHEN 'ZEILE_C'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '취소항번'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.

      WHEN 'WERKS_C'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '취소플랜트'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'MBLNR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재문서'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.
      WHEN 'MJAHR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '연도'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'ZEILE'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '항번'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'MATNR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.

      WHEN 'MAKTX'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '자재내역'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'IDNRK'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '구성품'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.

      WHEN 'OJTXB'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '구성품내역'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'BWART'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '이동유형'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'BUDAT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '처리일자'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'AUFNR'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '생산오더'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_font_underlined.

      WHEN 'CHARG'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '생산Lot번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'VFDAT'. "2021.05.18 추가
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '유효기간'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'ZLOT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '배치'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'PRUEFLOS'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '검사로트번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'AUSP1'.
        <ls_fcat>-col_pos = 22.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = 'Old 배치번호'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.

      WHEN 'LGORT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '출고저장위치'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'MENGE'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '출고수량'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-qfieldname = 'MEINS'.
      WHEN 'ERFME'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '단위'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'SGTXT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '비고'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'BDMNG'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '요청수량'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'SEL_FLAG'  OR 'HANDLE_STYLE'.
        <ls_fcat>-tech = gc_x.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_fcat BY col_pos fieldname.

ENDFORM.

*&      --> E_INTERACTIVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM create_toolbar_button7  USING po_object
                                TYPE REF TO cl_alv_event_toolbar_set
                       pv_interactive.

  DATA: ls_toolbar  TYPE stb_button.
  CLEAR ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   3               TO   ls_toolbar-butn_type.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.

  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZINPC'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_previous_node  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '생산투입취소'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.


  "2021.04.27 생산투입의 칭량라벨 발행 버튼 추가
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL2'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.


  "2021.05.21 - 생산투입처럼  칭량라벨일괄발행 추가
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL3'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨일괄발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.


  "2021.07.06 칭량라벨 분할 발행
  CLEAR  ls_toolbar.
  MOVE   'ZLABEL5'          TO   ls_toolbar-function.
  MOVE   icon_system_print  TO   ls_toolbar-icon.
  MOVE   '칭량라벨분할발행'     TO   ls_toolbar-text.
  APPEND ls_toolbar      TO   po_object->mt_toolbar.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_GOODSMVT_CANCEL2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_goodsmvt_cancel2 .

  DATA : lt_field  LIKE TABLE OF sval WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_return.

  DATA lv_matdoc    TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-mat_doc.
  DATA lv_mjahr     TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-doc_year.
  DATA lv_budat     TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_02-pstng_date.
  DATA ls_goodsmvt_headret    TYPE bapi2017_gm_head_ret.


  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_inpc-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_inpc.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_inpc-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_inpc INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.
  ENDLOOP.

  "2021.04.22 팝업창 비활성화처리
*  _CLS LT_FIELD.
*  "출고일자
*  LT_FIELD-TABNAME = 'MSEG'.
*  LT_FIELD-FIELDNAME = 'BUDAT_MKPF'.
*  LT_FIELD-FIELDTEXT = '취소일자'.
*  LT_FIELD-VALUE = ''.
*  APPEND LT_FIELD. CLEAR LT_FIELD.
*
*  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
*    EXPORTING
*      POPUP_TITLE     = '투입취소 일자를 입력하세요'
*      START_COLUMN    = '50'
*      START_ROW       = '10'
*    IMPORTING
*      RETURNCODE      = LV_RETURN
*    TABLES
*      FIELDS          = LT_FIELD
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      ERROR_IN_FIELDS = 1
*      OTHERS          = 2.
*
*  IF SY-SUBRC <> 0 OR LV_RETURN = 'A' OR LT_FIELD-VALUE IS INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE S008.
*    GV_ANSWER = GC_N.
*    EXIT.
*  ENDIF.
*
*
*  CLEAR GV_BUDAT.
*  READ TABLE LT_FIELD INDEX 1.
*  GV_BUDAT = LT_FIELD-VALUE.

*  CHECK GV_BUDAT IS NOT INITIAL.

  LOOP AT gt_inpc WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
                     AND mblnr_c IS INITIAL.
    "2021.06.09 - 오더상태가 TECO는 투입취소 안되도록 처리
    DATA(lv_objnr) = 'OR' && gt_inpc-aufnr.

    SELECT SINGLE stat INTO jest-stat
      FROM jest
     WHERE objnr EQ lv_objnr
       AND stat EQ 'I0045'
       AND inact EQ space.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gt_inpc-icon = gc_err.
      gt_inpc-msg = TEXT-m11.
      MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING icon msg .
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.


    lv_matdoc = gt_inpc-mblnr.
    lv_mjahr = gt_inpc-mjahr.
*    LV_BUDAT = GT_INPC-BUDAT.
    CLEAR lv_budat.
    SELECT SINGLE budat_mkpf INTO lv_budat
      FROM mseg
     WHERE mblnr EQ lv_matdoc
       AND mjahr EQ lv_mjahr.


    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_GOODSMVT_CANCEL'
      EXPORTING
        materialdocument    = lv_matdoc
        matdocumentyear     = lv_mjahr
        goodsmvt_pstng_date = lv_budat
      IMPORTING
        goodsmvt_headret    = ls_goodsmvt_headret
      TABLES
        return              = gt_return.


    READ TABLE gt_return WITH KEY type = gc_e.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gt_inpc-icon = gc_err.

      IF gt_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        gt_inpc-msg = gt_return[ type = gc_e ]-message.
      ENDIF.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
    ELSE.

      gt_inpc-icon = gc_suc.
      gt_inpc-msg = TEXT-m03.

      gt_inpc-mblnr_c = ls_goodsmvt_headret-mat_doc.
      gt_inpc-mjahr_c = ls_goodsmvt_headret-doc_year.
      gt_inpc-zeile_c = '0001'.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
        EXPORTING
          wait = gc_x.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING icon msg mblnr_c mjahr_c zeile_c werks_c.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GET_DATA7
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_data7 .

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_ent OCCURS 0,
          werks LIKE marc-werks,
          matnr LIKE marc-matnr,
          lgort LIKE marc-lgpro,
        END OF lt_ent.

  _cls gt_mov.
  SELECT DISTINCT
          rs~rsnum, "예약번호
          rs~rspos, "예약항번
          rs~bwart, "이동유형
          rs~bdter, "입고요청일
          rs~werks, "플랜트
          rs~matnr, "구성품
          mt~maktx, "구성품 내역
          rs~lgort, "출고저장위치

          "2021.06.08 ERFMNG->BDMNG 변경
*          RS~ERFMG, "요청수량
          rs~bdmng AS erfmg,

          rs~enmng, "기출고수량
          rs~meins, "단위
          rf~umlgo, "입고저장위치
          rs~sgtxt, "비고
          rs~charg, "배치
          ma~xchpf  "배치관리 지시자
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @gt_mov
    FROM resb AS rs INNER JOIN rkpf AS rf
                               ON rf~rsnum EQ rs~rsnum
                     INNER JOIN mara AS ma
                              ON rs~matnr EQ ma~matnr
                     LEFT JOIN makt AS mt
                              ON ma~matnr EQ mt~matnr
                             AND mt~spras EQ @sy-langu
*                     INNER JOIN MARC AS MC
*                              ON RS~MATNR EQ MC~MATNR
*                             AND RS~UMWRK EQ MC~WERKS
   WHERE rs~werks EQ @p_werks"플랜트
*     AND RS~UMLGO EQ @P_LGORT"저장위치
     AND rf~rsdat IN @s_grdat"입고요청일
     AND ma~mtart IN @s_mtart "2021.04.15 자재유형 추가
     AND rs~lgort IN @s_lgpro "2021.04.15 출고저장위치 추가
     AND rs~bwart EQ 'Z41'
     AND rs~xloek EQ @space
     AND rs~kzear EQ @space.

  _cls lt_ent.
  lt_ent[] = VALUE #( BASE lt_ent[] FOR ls_mov IN gt_mov
                        ( CORRESPONDING #( ls_mov ) ) ).

  SORT lt_ent BY werks matnr lgort.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ent COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

  IF lt_ent[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    "2021.06.08 조건에 상관없이 저장위치 재고로 발췌

    "배치관리 안하는 자재의 재고 발췌 (MARD)
    SELECT DISTINCT
            md~werks,
            md~matnr,
            md~lgort,
            md~labst
       INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mard)
       FROM mard AS md INNER JOIN mara AS ma
                                 ON md~matnr EQ ma~matnr
                        INNER JOIN makt AS mt
                                 ON ma~matnr EQ mt~matnr
                                AND mt~spras EQ @sy-langu
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_ent
     WHERE md~werks EQ @lt_ent-werks
       AND md~matnr EQ @lt_ent-matnr
       AND md~lgort EQ @lt_ent-lgort
*       AND MA~XCHPF EQ @SPACE         "2021.06.08 주석
       AND md~labst NE 0.
    SORT lt_mard BY werks matnr lgort.

    ""2021.06.08 주석
    "BEGIN
*    "배치관리 하는 자재의 배치재고 발췌 (MCHB)
*    SELECT DISTINCT
*            MH~WERKS,
*            MH~MATNR,
*            MH~LGORT,
*            MH~CHARG,
*            MH~CLABS AS LABST
*       INTO TABLE @DATA(LT_MCHB)
*       FROM MCHB AS MH INNER JOIN MARA AS MA
*                                 ON MH~MATNR EQ MA~MATNR
*                        INNER JOIN MAKT AS MT
*                                 ON MA~MATNR EQ MT~MATNR
*                                AND MT~SPRAS EQ @SY-LANGU
*      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @LT_ENT
*     WHERE MH~WERKS EQ @LT_ENT-WERKS
*       AND MH~MATNR EQ @LT_ENT-MATNR
*       AND MH~LGORT EQ @LT_ENT-LGORT
*       AND MA~XCHPF EQ @GC_X
*       AND MH~CLABS NE 0.
*    SORT LT_MCHB BY WERKS MATNR LGORT CHARG.
    "END
  ENDIF.


  "출고저장위치 재고
  LOOP AT gt_mov ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_mov>).
*    IF <LS_MOV>-XCHPF EQ SPACE.   "2021.06.08 주석
    READ TABLE lt_mard INTO DATA(ls_mard) WITH KEY werks = <ls_mov>-werks
                                                       matnr = <ls_mov>-matnr
                                                       lgort = <ls_mov>-lgort
                                                       BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      <ls_mov>-labst = ls_mard-labst.
    ENDIF.
    "2021.06.08 주석
    "BEGIN
*    ELSE.
*      READ TABLE LT_MCHB INTO DATA(LS_MCHB) WITH KEY WERKS = <LS_MOV>-WERKS
*                                                        MATNR = <LS_MOV>-MATNR
*                                                        LGORT = <LS_MOV>-LGORT
*                                                        CHARG = <LS_MOV>-CHARG
*                                                   BINARY SEARCH.
*      IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
*        <LS_MOV>-LABST = LS_MCHB-LABST.
*      ENDIF.
*
*    ENDIF.
    "END

    "2021.04.15 추가 이미 311로 처리된 예약번호의 자재배치 값 디폴트 발췌
    DATA(lv_sgtxt) = <ls_mov>-rsnum && <ls_mov>-rspos.

    SELECT SINGLE charg INTO <ls_mov>-charg
      FROM resb
     WHERE sgtxt EQ lv_sgtxt.


*2021.06.04 필드추가
*화면의 RESB-RSNUM and RSPOS 로 SGTXT를 찾아서
*SGTXT의 RSNUM and RSPOS로  RESB-EBELN and EBELP를 찾아서
*EKPO-EBELN and EBELP로 MATNR and TXZ01을 가져와서 추가 필드에 보여준다.

    DATA(lv_rsnum) = <ls_mov>-sgtxt(10).
    DATA(lv_rspos) = <ls_mov>-sgtxt+10(4).

    SELECT SINGLE ep~ebeln
                    ep~ebelp
                    ep~matnr
                    ep~txz01
      INTO (<ls_mov>-ebeln, <ls_mov>-ebelp,<ls_mov>-matn2,<ls_mov>-txz01)
      FROM resb AS r INNER JOIN ekpo AS ep
                               ON r~ebeln EQ ep~ebeln
                              AND r~ebelp EQ ep~ebelp
    WHERE r~rsnum EQ lv_rsnum
      AND r~rspos EQ lv_rspos.


*      2021.07.16 필드 추가
*      EBELN/EBELP -> get EKET-CHARG
    SELECT SINGLE charg
      FROM eket
     WHERE ebeln = @<ls_mov>-ebeln
       AND ebelp = @<ls_mov>-ebelp
      INTO @<ls_mov>-charg_e.

*        발주품목/배치번호 -> get MCH1-LICHA
    SELECT SINGLE licha
      FROM mch1
     WHERE matnr = @<ls_mov>-matn2
       AND charg = @<ls_mov>-charg_e
      INTO @<ls_mov>-licha.


  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_mov BY rsnum rspos matnr.

  IF gt_mov[] IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s004 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
  ELSE.
    gv_cnt = lines( gt_mov ).
    MESSAGE s001 WITH gv_cnt.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GRID_DOUBLE_CLICK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> E_ROW
*&      --> E_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM grid_double_click  USING es_row TYPE lvc_s_row
                             es_column TYPE lvc_s_col.

  DATA: BEGIN OF ls_trans,
          matnr   LIKE mara-matnr,
          aufnr   LIKE afko-aufnr,
          mblnr   LIKE mseg-mblnr,
          mjahr   LIKE mseg-mjahr,
          plnbez  LIKE mara-matnr,
          idnrk   LIKE mara-matnr,
          mblnr_c LIKE mseg-mblnr,
          mjahr_c LIKE mseg-mjahr,
        END OF ls_trans.

  CLEAR ls_trans.

  CASE gc_x.
    WHEN ra_req.              "자재요청
      CLEAR gt_req.
      READ TABLE gt_req INDEX es_row-index.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_req TO ls_trans.

    WHEN ra_chg.
      CLEAR gt_chg.           "변경
      READ TABLE gt_chg  INDEX es_row-index.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_chg TO ls_trans.

    WHEN ra_mov. "OR ra_movo.   "이동,  외주자재이동
      CLEAR gt_mov.
      READ TABLE gt_mov  INDEX es_row-index.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_mov TO ls_trans.

    WHEN ra_dis.              "조회
      CLEAR gt_dis.
      READ TABLE gt_dis  INDEX es_row-index.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_dis TO ls_trans.

*    WHEN ra_inp.              "생산투입
*      CLEAR gt_inp.
*      READ TABLE gt_inp  INDEX es_row-index.
*      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_inp TO ls_trans.
*
*    WHEN ra_inpc.             "생산투입취소
*      CLEAR gt_inpc.
*      READ TABLE gt_inpc  INDEX es_row-index.
*      MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_inpc TO ls_trans.

  ENDCASE.


  CHECK ls_trans IS NOT INITIAL.

  CASE es_column-fieldname.
    WHEN 'AUFNR'.
      CHECK ls_trans-aufnr IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET PARAMETER ID 'BR1' FIELD ls_trans-aufnr.
      CALL TRANSACTION 'COR3' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
    WHEN 'MATNR' OR 'IDNRK' OR 'PLNBEZ'.
      CHECK ls_trans-matnr IS NOT INITIAL.
      SET PARAMETER ID 'MAT' FIELD ls_trans-matnr.
      SET PARAMETER ID 'MXX' FIELD 'K'.
      CALL TRANSACTION 'MM03' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
    WHEN 'MBLNR'.
      CHECK ls_trans-mblnr IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL FUNCTION 'MIGO_DIALOG'
        EXPORTING
          i_mblnr             = ls_trans-mblnr
          i_mjahr             = ls_trans-mjahr
        EXCEPTIONS
          illegal_combination = 1
          OTHERS              = 2.
    WHEN 'MBLNR_C'.
      CHECK ls_trans-mblnr_c IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL FUNCTION 'MIGO_DIALOG'
        EXPORTING
          i_mblnr             = ls_trans-mblnr_c
          i_mjahr             = ls_trans-mjahr_c
        EXCEPTIONS
          illegal_combination = 1
          OTHERS              = 2.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GRID_DOUBLE_CLICK2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> E_ROW
*&      --> E_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM grid_double_click2  USING es_row TYPE lvc_s_row
                             es_column TYPE lvc_s_col.


  CLEAR gt_req.
  READ TABLE gt_req_sum INDEX es_row-index.

  CHECK gt_req_sum-matnr IS NOT INITIAL.

  CASE es_column-fieldname.
    WHEN 'MATNR'.
      SET PARAMETER ID 'MAT' FIELD gt_req_sum-matnr.
      SET PARAMETER ID 'MXX' FIELD 'K'.
      CALL TRANSACTION 'MM03' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form ASSIGN_BATCH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM assign_batch .

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_select OCCURS 0,
          matnr     LIKE resb-matnr,
          lgfsb     LIKE marc-lgfsb,
          rsnum     LIKE resb-rsnum,
          rspos     LIKE resb-rspos,
          input_qty LIKE resb-erfmg,
          meins     LIKE resb-meins,
          erfme     LIKE resb-erfme,
        END OF lt_select.

  DATA: ls_select LIKE lt_select.

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_result OCCURS 0.
          INCLUDE STRUCTURE lt_select.
  DATA:   charg LIKE mchb-charg,
          clabs LIKE mchb-clabs,
        END OF lt_result.

  DATA: lv_tot_qty LIKE mchb-clabs,
        ls_list    LIKE gt_mov,
        ls_celltab TYPE lvc_s_styl,
        lv_exit.


  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_mov-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_mov.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_mov-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_mov INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  _cls lt_select.
  LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
                    AND xchpf EQ gc_x.    "배치관리 지시자만 수행
    MOVE gt_mov-matnr TO lt_select-matnr. "자재번호
    MOVE gt_mov-lgort TO lt_select-lgfsb. "출고저장위치
    MOVE gt_mov-rsnum TO lt_select-rsnum. "예약번호 ,원소스에는 생산오더 단위
    MOVE gt_mov-rspos TO lt_select-rspos. "예약항번
    MOVE gt_mov-erfmg TO lt_select-input_qty. "요청수량
    MOVE gt_mov-meins TO lt_select-meins.
    MOVE gt_mov-meins TO lt_select-erfme.
    APPEND lt_select.
  ENDLOOP.


* 구성품+출고저장위치+오더번호
  SORT lt_select BY matnr lgfsb rsnum rspos.

  LOOP AT lt_select.
    ls_select = lt_select.

    AT NEW lgfsb.   "구성품+저장위치 재고
      PERFORM  GET_AVAILABLE_BATCH_STOCK USING lt_select-matnr
                                                 lt_select-lgfsb
                                                 ls_select-meins
                                                 ls_select-erfme
                                        CHANGING lv_tot_qty.
    ENDAT.

* 가용재고가 요청수량보다 작으면 배치 제안하지 않음.
    IF lv_tot_qty < lt_select-input_qty.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT gt_mchb WHERE matnr = lt_select-matnr
                      AND lgort = lt_select-lgfsb.
      CLEAR lt_result.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_select TO lt_result.
      lt_result-charg = gt_mchb-charg.

      IF gt_mchb-clabs < lt_select-input_qty.
        lt_result-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs.
        APPEND lt_result.

        lt_select-input_qty = lt_select-input_qty - lt_result-clabs.
        lv_tot_qty          = lv_tot_qty - lt_result-clabs.

        DELETE gt_mchb.
        CONTINUE.
      ELSE.
        lt_result-clabs = lt_select-input_qty.
        APPEND lt_result.

        gt_mchb-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs - lt_select-input_qty.
        MODIFY gt_mchb.

        lv_tot_qty    = lv_tot_qty - lt_result-clabs.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.


  SORT lt_result BY matnr lgfsb rsnum rspos charg.

  LOOP AT lt_result.
    AT NEW rspos.
      CLEAR ls_list.

      READ TABLE gt_mov WITH KEY rsnum = lt_result-rsnum
                                  matnr = lt_result-matnr
                                  rspos = lt_result-rspos.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_list = gt_mov.
        DELETE gt_mov INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.
    ENDAT.

    gt_mov = ls_list.
    gt_mov-charg = lt_result-charg.
    gt_mov-giqty = lt_result-clabs.
    APPEND gt_mov.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_mov BY rsnum rspos werks matnr charg.


ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form get_available_batch_stock
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> LT_SELECT_MATNR
*&      --> LT_SELECT_LGFSB
*&      <-- LV_TOT_QTY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_available_batch_stock  USING    p_matnr
                                         p_lgort
                                         p_meins
                                         p_erfme
                                CHANGING p_stk_qty.

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_resb OCCURS 0,
          charg LIKE resb-charg,
          meins LIKE resb-meins,
          bdmng LIKE resb-bdmng,
          enmng LIKE resb-enmng,
        END OF lt_resb.

  CLEAR: p_stk_qty, gt_mchb[].

  SELECT a~matnr,
         a~lgort,
         a~charg,
         a~clabs,
         b~lwedt
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @gt_mchb
    FROM mchb AS a LEFT OUTER JOIN mch1 AS b
      ON a~matnr = b~matnr
     AND a~charg = b~charg
   WHERE a~matnr = @p_matnr
     AND a~werks = @p_werks
     AND a~lgort = @p_lgort
     AND a~clabs > 0.

  gt_mchb-lwedt = '99991231'.
  MODIFY gt_mchb TRANSPORTING lwedt WHERE lwedt = '00000000'.

  SORT gt_mchb BY lwedt matnr lgort charg.

* 완료되지 않은 예약
  SELECT charg meins SUM( bdmng ) SUM( enmng )
    INTO TABLE lt_resb
    FROM resb
    WHERE bwart  = '311'
      AND xloek  = ' '
      AND kzear  = ' '
      AND werks  = p_werks
      AND lgort  = p_lgort
      AND matnr  = p_matnr
      GROUP BY charg meins.

* 모든 수량은 기본단위로 읽은 다음 예약 입력돤위로 환산함.
  LOOP AT gt_mchb.
    READ TABLE lt_resb WITH KEY charg = gt_mchb-charg.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gt_mchb-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs - ( lt_resb-bdmng - lt_resb-enmng ).
      IF gt_mchb-clabs < 0.
        DELETE gt_mchb. CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_meins NE p_erfme.
      PERFORM  MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT USING p_matnr
                                             p_meins
                                             p_erfme
                                    CHANGING gt_mchb-clabs.

    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_mchb.

    p_stk_qty = p_stk_qty + gt_mchb-clabs.
  ENDLOOP.

  DELETE gt_mchb WHERE clabs = 0.

  SORT gt_mchb BY lwedt matnr lgort charg.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> P_MATNR
*&      --> P_MEINS
*&      --> P_ERFME
*&      <-- GT_MCHB_CLABS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM md_convert_material_unit  USING pv_matnr
                                       pv_in_meins
                                       pv_out_meins
                            CHANGING pv_menge.

  DATA : lv_matnr  LIKE mara-matnr.
  DATA : lv_in_me  LIKE mara-meins.
  DATA : lv_out_me LIKE mara-meins.
  DATA : lv_menge  LIKE ekpo-menge.

  lv_matnr  = pv_matnr.
  lv_in_me  = pv_in_meins.
  lv_out_me = pv_out_meins.
  lv_menge  = pv_menge.

  CALL FUNCTION 'MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT'
    EXPORTING
      i_matnr              = lv_matnr
      i_in_me              = lv_in_me
      i_out_me             = lv_out_me
      i_menge              = lv_menge
    IMPORTING
      e_menge              = pv_menge
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_application = 1
      error                = 2
      OTHERS               = 3.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form ASSIGN_BATCH2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM assign_batch2 .

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_select OCCURS 0,
          matnr     LIKE resb-matnr,
          lgfsb     LIKE marc-lgfsb,
          rsnum     LIKE resb-rsnum,
          rspos     LIKE resb-rspos,
          input_qty LIKE resb-erfmg,
          meins     LIKE resb-meins,
          erfme     LIKE resb-erfme,
        END OF lt_select.

  DATA: ls_select LIKE lt_select.

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_result OCCURS 0.
          INCLUDE STRUCTURE lt_select.
  DATA:   charg LIKE mchb-charg,
          clabs LIKE mchb-clabs,
        END OF lt_result.

  DATA: lv_tot_qty LIKE mchb-clabs,
        ls_list    LIKE gt_inp,
        ls_celltab TYPE lvc_s_styl,
        lv_exit.


  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_inp-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_inp TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_inp.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_inp-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_inp INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  _cls lt_select.
  LOOP AT gt_inp WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
                    AND xchpf EQ gc_x.    "배치관리 지시자만 수행
    MOVE gt_inp-matnr TO lt_select-matnr. "자재번호
    MOVE gt_inp-lgort TO lt_select-lgfsb. "출고저장위치
    MOVE gt_inp-rsnum TO lt_select-rsnum. "예약번호 ,원소스에는 생산오더 단위
    MOVE gt_inp-rspos TO lt_select-rspos. "예약항번
    MOVE gt_inp-bdmng TO lt_select-input_qty. "요청수량
    MOVE gt_inp-meins TO lt_select-meins.
    MOVE gt_inp-meins TO lt_select-erfme.
    APPEND lt_select.
  ENDLOOP.


* 구성품+출고저장위치+예약번호
  SORT lt_select BY matnr lgfsb rsnum rspos.

  LOOP AT lt_select.
    ls_select = lt_select.

    AT NEW lgfsb.   "구성품+저장위치 재고
      PERFORM  GET_AVAILABLE_BATCH_STOCK2 USING lt_select-matnr
                                                 lt_select-lgfsb
                                                 ls_select-meins
                                                 ls_select-erfme
                                        CHANGING lv_tot_qty.
    ENDAT.

* 가용재고가 요청수량보다 작으면 배치 제안하지 않음.
    IF lv_tot_qty < lt_select-input_qty.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT gt_mchb WHERE matnr = lt_select-matnr
                      AND lgort = lt_select-lgfsb.
      CLEAR lt_result.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_select TO lt_result.
      lt_result-charg = gt_mchb-charg.

      IF gt_mchb-clabs < lt_select-input_qty.
        lt_result-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs.
        APPEND lt_result.

        lt_select-input_qty = lt_select-input_qty - lt_result-clabs.
        lv_tot_qty          = lv_tot_qty - lt_result-clabs.

        DELETE gt_mchb.
        CONTINUE.
      ELSE.
        lt_result-clabs = lt_select-input_qty.
        APPEND lt_result.

        gt_mchb-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs - lt_select-input_qty.
        MODIFY gt_mchb.

        lv_tot_qty    = lv_tot_qty - lt_result-clabs.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.


  SORT lt_result BY matnr lgfsb rsnum rspos charg.

  LOOP AT lt_result.
    AT NEW rspos.
      CLEAR ls_list.

      READ TABLE gt_inp WITH KEY rsnum = lt_result-rsnum
                                  matnr = lt_result-matnr
                                  rspos = lt_result-rspos.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_list = gt_inp.
        DELETE gt_inp INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.
    ENDAT.

    gt_inp = ls_list.
    gt_inp-zlot = lt_result-charg.
    gt_inp-erfmg = lt_result-clabs.


    "2021.05.18 구성부품의 배치에 대한 유효기간 필드 추가(MCH1-VFDAT)
    SELECT SINGLE vfdat INTO gt_inp-vfdat
      FROM mch1
     WHERE matnr EQ gt_inp-matnr
       AND charg EQ gt_inp-zlot
       AND lvorm EQ space.

    "2021.05.18 검사로트번호 발췌
*자재코드/배치번호를 Key로 QALS의 테이블을 조회한다.(ART는 0110(외주검사), 04(생산입고검사), 05(무상입고검사)에 대한 전부 리스트 가져온다.)
*기존으로 조회한다음 QALS-OBJNR의 값을 기준으로 상태 TALBLE JEST를 찾아서 I0224(로트취소)가 아닌 대상의 검사로트 번호를 화면에 보여준다.

    "품질검사로트
    SELECT SINGLE qa~prueflos
      INTO gt_inp-prueflos
      FROM qals AS qa
     WHERE art IN ( '0110', '04' , '05', '01' )
       AND qa~matnr EQ gt_inp-matnr
       AND qa~charg EQ gt_inp-zlot
       AND NOT EXISTS
            ( SELECT *
                 FROM jest
                WHERE objnr EQ qa~objnr
                  AND stat EQ 'I0224'
                  AND inact EQ '' ).



    "2021.05.18 old batch 값 발췌

    PERFORM   GET_OLD_BATCH_CLASSFICATION USING gt_inp-werks
                                                 gt_inp-matnr
                                                 gt_inp-zlot
                                          CHANGING gt_inp-ausp1.

    APPEND gt_inp.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_inp BY rsnum rspos werks matnr charg.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_RESERVATION_CREATE1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_reservation_create1 .
  DATA : lt_field  LIKE TABLE OF sval WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_return.

  DATA ls_head    TYPE bapi2093_res_head.
  DATA lv_rsnum             TYPE bapi2093_res_key-reserv_no.
  DATA lt_item              TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi2093_res_item.
  DATA ls_item              TYPE bapi2093_res_item.

  DATA lt_seg                  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi_profitability_segment.
  DATA lt_return               TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2.
  DATA lt_extension          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiparex.
  DATA ls_extension           TYPE bapiparex.

*  DATA ls_zmms0070 TYPE zmms0070.
  DATA lv_rspos LIKE resb-rspos.
  DATA lt_ext_res TYPE bapiparex_tab.
  DATA lt_0061 TYPE TABLE OF zdmmt0090 WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA lv_wempf LIKE rkpf-wempf. "사원번호
  DATA lv_budat LIKE mseg-budat_mkpf. "입고요청일

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_head OCCURS 0,
*          LGFSB LIKE MARC-LGFSB,
          aufnr  LIKE afko-aufnr, "생산 인 경우
          zlifnr LIKE resb-lifnr, "외주 인 경우
        END OF lt_head.

  DATA lv_cond TYPE string.

  _cls lt_field.
  "사원번호
  lt_field-tabname = 'RKPF'.
  lt_field-fieldname = 'WEMPF'.
  lt_field-fieldtext = '사원번호'.
  lt_field-value = ''.
  APPEND lt_field. CLEAR lt_field.
  "입고일자
  lt_field-tabname = 'MSEG'.
  lt_field-fieldname = 'BUDAT_MKPF'.
  lt_field-fieldtext = '입고요청일'.
  lt_field-value = ''.
  APPEND lt_field. CLEAR lt_field.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      popup_title     = '입고요청일을 입력하세요'
      start_column    = '50'
      start_row       = '10'
    IMPORTING
      returncode      = lv_return
    TABLES
      fields          = lt_field
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_fields = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR lv_return = 'A' OR lt_field-value IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s008.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.


  CLEAR: lv_wempf, lv_budat.
  READ TABLE lt_field INDEX 1.
  lv_wempf = lt_field-value.
  READ TABLE lt_field INDEX 2.
  lv_budat = lt_field-value.


  CHECK lv_wempf IS NOT INITIAL AND
         lv_budat IS NOT INITIAL.

  _cls lt_head.
  LOOP AT gt_req_sum WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
    "2021.03.31 헤더 기준 데이터를 생산/외주별 분리
    CASE p_reqty.
      WHEN '261'.
        MOVE gt_req_sum-aufnr TO lt_head-aufnr.
      WHEN 'Z41'.
        MOVE gt_req_sum-zlifnr TO lt_head-zlifnr.
    ENDCASE.
    _app lt_head.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR lv_cond.
  CASE p_reqty.
    WHEN '261'.
      SORT lt_head BY aufnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_head COMPARING aufnr.

    WHEN 'Z41'.
      SORT lt_head BY zlifnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_head COMPARING zlifnr.

  ENDCASE.

  CHECK lt_head[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  "Reservation Header
  LOOP AT lt_head.
    CLEAR:  ls_head, lv_rspos.
    ls_head-res_date = lv_budat.
    ls_head-move_type  = '311'.
    ls_head-move_plant = p_werks.
    ls_head-move_stloc = p_lgort.
    ls_head-orderid = lt_head-aufnr.

    REFRESH  lt_item.
    CLEAR lv_rspos.
    "2021.03.31  아이템텍스트 생산과 외주 구분하여 입력

    CASE p_reqty.
      WHEN '261'.
        LOOP AT gt_req_sum WHERE sel_flag = gc_x AND aufnr = lt_head-aufnr.
          ADD 1 TO  lv_rspos.
          CLEAR ls_item.
          ls_item-material = gt_req_sum-matnr.
          ls_item-plant    = gt_req_sum-werks.
          ls_item-stge_loc = gt_req_sum-lgpro.

          IF gt_req_sum-zlot IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_item-batch    = gt_req_sum-zlot.
          ENDIF.

          ls_item-entry_qnt = gt_req_sum-erfmg.
          ls_item-movement = gc_x.
          ls_item-gr_rcpt = lv_wempf.

          READ TABLE gt_reqty WITH KEY key = p_reqty.
          ls_item-unload_pt = gt_reqty-text(25).

          ls_item-item_text = gt_req_sum-rsnum_old && gt_req_sum-rspos_old.
          APPEND ls_item TO lt_item.

        ENDLOOP.
      WHEN 'Z41'.
        LOOP AT gt_req_sum WHERE sel_flag = gc_x AND zlifnr = lt_head-zlifnr.
          ADD 1 TO  lv_rspos.
          CLEAR ls_item.
          ls_item-material = gt_req_sum-matnr.
          ls_item-plant    = gt_req_sum-werks.
          ls_item-stge_loc = gt_req_sum-lgpro.
          IF gt_req_sum-zlot IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_item-batch    = gt_req_sum-zlot.
          ENDIF.
          ls_item-entry_qnt = gt_req_sum-erfmg.
          ls_item-movement = gc_x.
          ls_item-gr_rcpt = lv_wempf.

          READ TABLE gt_reqty WITH KEY key = p_reqty.
          ls_item-unload_pt = gt_reqty-text(25).

          ls_item-item_text = gt_req_sum-zlifnr.
          APPEND ls_item TO lt_item.

        ENDLOOP.
    ENDCASE.

    REFRESH lt_return.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_RESERVATION_CREATE1'
      EXPORTING
        reservationheader    = ls_head
      IMPORTING
        reservation          = lv_rsnum
      TABLES
        reservationitems     = lt_item
        profitabilitysegment = lt_seg
        return               = lt_return
        extensionin          = lt_extension.


    IF lv_rsnum IS INITIAL.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.

      CLEAR gt_req_sum.
      gt_req_sum-icon = gc_err.
      IF lt_return[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        gt_req_sum-msg = lt_return[ type = gc_e ]-message.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY gt_req_sum TRANSPORTING icon msg
                           WHERE (lv_cond).

    ELSE.

      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
        EXPORTING
          wait = gc_x.

      _cls lt_0061.
      CLEAR lv_rspos.
      LOOP AT gt_req_sum WHERE (lv_cond).
        gt_req_sum-icon = gc_suc.
        gt_req_sum-msg = TEXT-m03.
        ADD 1 TO lv_rspos.
        gt_req_sum-rsnum = lv_rsnum.

        gt_req_sum-rspos = lv_rspos.
        MODIFY gt_req_sum TRANSPORTING rsnum rspos icon msg.


        MOVE gt_req_sum-rsnum_old TO lt_0061-rsnum.
        MOVE gt_req_sum-rspos_old TO lt_0061-rspos.

        MOVE gt_req_sum-zlifnr TO lt_0061-lifnr.

        MOVE sy-datum TO lt_0061-erdat.
        MOVE sy-uzeit TO lt_0061-erzet.
        MOVE sy-uname TO lt_0061-ernam.
        _app lt_0061.


      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  IF lt_0061[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    MODIFY zdmmt0090 FROM TABLE lt_0061.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

  DATA: BEGIN OF ls_movo,
          txz01 LIKE ekpo-txz01,

  IF lines( gt_rows[] ) > 1.  "AND
*      ( ra_inp EQ gc_x OR

    MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-e07 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e. "하나의 행만 선택 가능합니다.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CASE gc_x.
    WHEN ra_mov.                             "이동
      "기존 선택값 해제
      CLEAR gt_mov-sel_flag.
      MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                        WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

*      "선택값 설정

*      "기존 선택값 해제

*      MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING sel_flag
*                        WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

*        CHECK ls_movo IS NOT INITIAL.

*        MOVE ls_movo-baugr TO gs_inp-plnbez.  "제품코드  제조번호 발췌용
*        MOVE ls_movo-werks TO gs_inp-werks.   "플랜트  제조번호 발췌용

*        MOVE ls_movo-txz01 TO gs_inp-maktx . "제품명
*        MOVE ls_movo-maktx TO gs_inp-matnr_t. "원료명 (구성품 내역)

FORM modify_excel_data .

  DATA lv_tot_cnt TYPE i.
