  _cls gt_excel_01.
  CLEAR: lv_tot, lv_tot_cnt, lv_page.
  LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

    IF lv_rsnum NE gt_mov-rsnum.
      ADD 1 TO lv_tot.  "총 페이지  ,예약번호만큼 페이지 생성
      ADD 1 TO lv_page. "현재페이지
      lv_rsnum = gt_mov-rsnum.
      CLEAR lv_cnt.
    ENDIF.

    ADD 1 TO lv_cnt .
    ADD 1 TO lv_tot_cnt .

    "예약번호
    gt_excel_01-rsnum = |{ gt_mov-rsnum ALPHA = OUT }|.

    "플랜트/플랜트명
    SELECT SINGLE name1 INTO @DATA(lv_name1)
      FROM t001w
     WHERE werks EQ @gt_mov-werks.

    gt_excel_01-plant = gt_mov-werks && ` - ` && lv_name1.

    "바코드
    gt_excel_01-barcode = gt_excel_01-rsnum.

    "제품명/ 제조번호
    SELECT SINGLE mt~maktx
                    ap~charg
      INTO (gt_excel_01-maktx, gt_excel_01-charg)
      FROM resb AS rs INNER JOIN afpo AS ap
                                ON rs~aufnr EQ ap~aufnr
                       INNER JOIN makt AS mt
                                ON ap~matnr EQ mt~matnr
                               AND mt~spras EQ sy-langu
    WHERE rsnum EQ gt_mov-rsnum
       AND rspos EQ gt_mov-rspos.

    "입고 저장위치
    SELECT SINGLE lgobe INTO @DATA(lv_lgort)
      FROM t001l
     WHERE werks EQ @gt_mov-werks
       AND lgort EQ @gt_mov-umlgo .

    gt_excel_01-lgort = gt_mov-umlgo && ` - ` && lv_lgort.


    "입고요청일
    gt_excel_01-rsdat = gt_mov-bdter(4) && '.' &&
                        gt_mov-bdter+4(2) && '.' &&
                        gt_mov-bdter+6(2).

    "NO
    gt_excel_01-rspos = gt_mov-rspos.

    "품목코드
    gt_excel_01-matnr = |{ gt_mov-matnr ALPHA = OUT }|.

    "품목내역
    gt_excel_01-maktx2 = gt_mov-maktx.

    "배치번호
    gt_excel_01-batch = gt_mov-charg.

    "수량
    gt_excel_01-qty = gt_mov-giqty.
*    WRITE GT_MOV-GIQTY  TO GT_EXCEL_01-QTY UNIT GT_MOV-MEINS.

    "단위
    gt_excel_01-meins = gt_mov-meins.

    "출고 저장위치
    gt_excel_01-lgpro = gt_mov-lgort.

    gt_excel_01-zline = lv_cnt.         "일련번호



    IF lv_cnt > 22.    "예약번호에 해당하는 원자재가 22개 이상이면 페이지 수 1 증가
      ADD 1 TO lv_tot.
      ADD 1 TO lv_page.
    ENDIF.

    gt_excel_01-page = lv_page.

    APPEND gt_excel_01.
    CLEAR gt_excel_01.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_excel_01.
    gt_excel_01-tot_page = lv_tot.    " 페이지 수
    gt_excel_01-tot_cnt = lv_tot_cnt. " 전체 라인수
    MODIFY gt_excel_01.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form INIT_FACTORY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> P_
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM init_factory USING pv_val.
  IF factory IS INITIAL.

    CALL METHOD c_oi_factory_creator=>get_document_factory
      IMPORTING
        factory = factory
        retcode = retcode.

    IF retcode NE c_oi_errors=>ret_ok. EXIT. ENDIF.

    CALL METHOD factory->start_factory
      EXPORTING
        r3_application_name = pv_val
      IMPORTING
        retcode             = retcode.

    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
      EXPORTING
        type = 'E'.

    CALL METHOD factory->get_link_server
      IMPORTING
        link_server = link_server
        retcode     = retcode.

    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
      EXPORTING
        type = 'E'.

    CALL METHOD link_server->start_link_server
      IMPORTING
        retcode = retcode.

    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
      EXPORTING
        type = 'E'.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form OPEN_DOC2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM open_doc2 USING pv_val .

  DEFINE m_link_tab.
    CALL METHOD link_server->add_table_item2
      EXPORTING
        item_name  = &1
      IMPORTING
        retcode    = retcode
      CHANGING
        data_table = &2.
    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message EXPORTING type = 'E'.
  END-OF-DEFINITION.

  DATA lv_obj(30).

  lv_obj = sy-repid && pv_val.

  IF NOT link_server IS INITIAL.
    m_link_tab 'GT_EXCEL_01'  gt_excel_01[].
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_OI_LOAD_MIME_DATA'
    EXPORTING
      object_id        = lv_obj
    IMPORTING
      data_size        = doc_size
      document_format  = doc_format
      document_type    = doc_type
    TABLES
      data_table       = doc_table
    EXCEPTIONS
      object_not_found = 1
      internal_error   = 2
      OTHERS           = 3.

  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid   TYPE 'E' NUMBER   sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

  IF doc_size NE 0.
    CALL METHOD factory->get_document_proxy
      EXPORTING
        document_type  = doc_type
      IMPORTING
        document_proxy = document_proxy
        retcode        = retcode.

    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
      EXPORTING
        type = 'E'.

    CALL METHOD document_proxy->open_document_from_table
      EXPORTING
        document_table = doc_table[]
        document_size  = doc_size
      IMPORTING
        retcode        = retcode.
    CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
      EXPORTING
        type = 'E'.
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE e000 WITH '문서가 없습니다.'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form MACRO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> P_
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM macro  USING p_mode.


  CASE p_mode.
    WHEN '1'.
*      CALL METHOD DOCUMENT_PROXY->EXECUTE_MACRO
*        EXPORTING
*          MACRO_STRING = 'R3_MACRO_PRINT'
*        IMPORTING
*          RETCODE      = RETCODE.
*      CALL METHOD C_OI_ERRORS=>SHOW_MESSAGE
*        EXPORTING
*          TYPE = 'E'.

    WHEN '2'.
      CALL METHOD document_proxy->execute_macro
        EXPORTING
          macro_string = 'MACRO1'
        IMPORTING
          retcode      = retcode.
      CALL METHOD c_oi_errors=>show_message
        EXPORTING
          type = 'E'.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form CLOSE_FACTORY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM close_factory .
  IF NOT link_server IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD link_server->stop_link_server
      IMPORTING
        retcode = retcode.
    FREE link_server.
  ENDIF.

  IF NOT factory IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD factory->stop_factory
      IMPORTING
        retcode = retcode.
    FREE factory.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GET_AVAILABLE_BATCH_STOCK2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> LT_SELECT_MATNR
*&      --> LT_SELECT_LGFSB
*&      --> LS_SELECT_MEINS
*&      --> LS_SELECT_ERFME
*&      <-- LV_TOT_QTY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_available_batch_stock2  USING    p_matnr
                                         p_lgort
                                         p_meins
                                         p_erfme
                                CHANGING p_stk_qty.

  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_resb OCCURS 0,
          charg LIKE resb-charg,
          meins LIKE resb-meins,
          bdmng LIKE resb-bdmng,
          enmng LIKE resb-enmng,
        END OF lt_resb.

  CLEAR: p_stk_qty, gt_mchb[].

  SELECT a~matnr,
         a~lgort,
         a~charg,
         a~clabs,
         b~lwedt
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @gt_mchb
    FROM mchb AS a LEFT OUTER JOIN mch1 AS b
      ON a~matnr = b~matnr
     AND a~charg = b~charg
   WHERE a~matnr = @p_matnr
     AND a~werks = @p_werks
     AND a~lgort = @p_lgort
     AND a~clabs > 0.

  gt_mchb-lwedt = '99991231'.
  MODIFY gt_mchb TRANSPORTING lwedt WHERE lwedt = '00000000'.

  SORT gt_mchb BY lwedt matnr lgort charg.

* 완료되지 않은 예약
  SELECT charg meins SUM( bdmng ) SUM( enmng )
    INTO TABLE lt_resb
    FROM resb
    WHERE bwart  = '261'
      AND xloek  = ' '
      AND kzear  = ' '
      AND werks  = p_werks
      AND lgort  = p_lgort
      AND matnr  = p_matnr
      GROUP BY charg meins.

* 모든 수량은 기본단위로 읽은 다음 예약 입력단위로 환산함.
  LOOP AT gt_mchb.
    READ TABLE lt_resb WITH KEY charg = gt_mchb-charg.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gt_mchb-clabs = gt_mchb-clabs - ( lt_resb-bdmng - lt_resb-enmng ).
      IF gt_mchb-clabs < 0.
        DELETE gt_mchb. CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_meins NE p_erfme.
      PERFORM  MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT USING p_matnr
                                             p_meins
                                             p_erfme
                                    CHANGING gt_mchb-clabs.

    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_mchb.

    p_stk_qty = p_stk_qty + gt_mchb-clabs.
  ENDLOOP.

  DELETE gt_mchb WHERE clabs = 0.

  SORT gt_mchb BY lwedt matnr lgort charg.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form CALL_SMARTFORMS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> P_
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM call_smartforms  USING pv_ucomm.

* Internal table declaration
  DATA: lt_docs      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF docs,
        lt_lines     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline,
        lt_lines_sum TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline.

* Declaration of local variables.
  DATA: ls_job_output_info      TYPE ssfcrescl,
        ls_document_output_info TYPE ssfcrespd,
        ls_job_output_options   TYPE ssfcresop,
        ls_output_options       TYPE ssfcompop,
*        LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS   TYPE SSFCTRLOP,
        lv_e_devtype            TYPE rspoptype,
        lv_bin_filesize         TYPE i,
        lv_name                 TYPE string,
        lv_path                 TYPE string,
        lv_fullpath             TYPE string,
        lv_filter               TYPE string,
        lv_uact                 TYPE i,
        lv_guiobj               TYPE REF TO cl_gui_frontend_services,
        lv_filename             TYPE string,
        lv_fm_name              TYPE rs38l_fnam.

  DATA : lv_prtsetup VALUE 'X', "프린트설정화면 DISPLAY여부
         lv_user.               "USER SETTING 사용여부

  DATA : lv_formname TYPE tdsfname.

  DATA: lt_mov LIKE TABLE OF gt_mov WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA : lv_cnt TYPE i.
  DATA : lv_count TYPE i.
  DATA : lv_erfmg LIKE resb-erfmg.

  CASE gc_x.
    WHEN ra_mov.                           "이동

      lt_mov[] = gt_mov[].
      DELETE lt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ space.
      SORT lt_mov BY rsnum.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_mov COMPARING rsnum.

      LOOP AT lt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
        _cls gt_forms1.
        CLEAR gs_head.
        LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
                           AND rsnum EQ lt_mov-rsnum.

          CLEAR gt_forms1.

          gt_forms1-rsnum = |{ gt_mov-rsnum ALPHA = IN }|.
          gt_forms1-rspos = |{ gt_mov-rspos ALPHA = IN }|.

          "플랜트/플랜트명
          SELECT SINGLE name1 INTO @DATA(lv_name1)
            FROM t001w
           WHERE werks EQ @gt_mov-werks.

          gt_forms1-plant = gt_mov-werks && ` - ` && lv_name1.

          gt_forms1-barcode = gt_mov-rsnum.
          "제품명/ 제조번호
          SELECT SINGLE mt~maktx
                          m1~licha
            INTO (gt_forms1-maktx, gt_forms1-charg)
            FROM resb AS rs INNER JOIN afpo AS ap
                                      ON rs~aufnr EQ ap~aufnr
                             INNER JOIN makt AS mt
                                      ON ap~matnr EQ mt~matnr
                                     AND mt~spras EQ sy-langu
                             INNER JOIN mch1 AS m1
                                      ON ap~matnr EQ m1~matnr
                                     AND ap~charg EQ m1~charg
          WHERE rsnum EQ gt_mov-rsnum
             AND rspos EQ gt_mov-rspos.

          "입고 저장위치
          SELECT SINGLE lgobe INTO @DATA(lv_lgort)
            FROM t001l
           WHERE werks EQ @gt_mov-werks
             AND lgort EQ @gt_mov-umlgo .

          gt_forms1-lgort = gt_mov-umlgo && ` - ` && lv_lgort.

          "입고요청일
          gt_forms1-rsdat = gt_mov-bdter(4) && '.' &&
                              gt_mov-bdter+4(2) && '.' &&
                              gt_mov-bdter+6(2).

          "품목코드
          gt_forms1-matnr = |{ gt_mov-matnr ALPHA = OUT }|.

          "품목내역
          gt_forms1-maktx2 = gt_mov-maktx.

          "배치번호
          gt_forms1-batch = gt_mov-charg.

          "수량
          WRITE gt_mov-giqty TO gt_forms1-qty UNIT gt_mov-meins RIGHT-JUSTIFIED.

          "단위
          gt_forms1-meins = gt_mov-meins.

          "출고 저장위치
          gt_forms1-lgpro = gt_mov-lgort.
          APPEND gt_forms1.

          MOVE-CORRESPONDING gt_forms1 TO gs_head.

        ENDLOOP.


        DATA: l_idex TYPE i,
              l_mod  TYPE i,
              l_do   TYPE i.

        l_mod = lines( gt_forms1 ) MOD 25 .

        l_do = 25 - l_mod.

        DO l_do TIMES.
          CLEAR  gt_forms1.
          APPEND gt_forms1.
        ENDDO.

        lv_formname = 'ZSFPP_0001'.

        "===================================================================
*호출할 스마트폼 명

        CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
          EXPORTING
            formname = lv_formname
          IMPORTING
            fm_name  = lv_fm_name.

*    LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS-TDDEST        = 'ZSAPGUI'. " 'LP01'.
        ls_output_options-tdnewid       = 'X'.
        ls_output_options-tdimmed       = 'X'.
        ls_output_options-tddelete      = 'X'.       " 'X' after tests
        ls_output_options-tdlifetime    = '0'.       " '0' days after test
        ls_output_options-tdcover       = ' '.       " no cover sheet
        ls_output_options-tdcopies      = '001'.

*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_DIALOG = 'X'.    "출력옵션 화면
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-PREVIEW   = 'X'.   " 미리보기
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-LANGU     = SY-LANGU.

*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_DIALOG = 'X'.
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_OPEN   = 'X'.
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_CLOSE  = 'X'.
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-PREVIEW   = ''.

*  IF RB_02 EQ GC_X. "PDF 선택 시
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_DIALOG = 'X'.
*    LS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-GETOTF = 'X'.
*  ENDIF.
*스마트 폼 호출.
*(최종 내부테이블 및 변수 넘김)
        CLEAR: ls_document_output_info,
                ls_job_output_info,
                ls_job_output_options.

        CALL FUNCTION lv_fm_name
          EXPORTING
            control_parameters   = gs_control_parameters
            output_options       = ls_output_options
            user_setting         = lv_user
            gs_head              = gs_head
          IMPORTING
            document_output_info = ls_document_output_info
            job_output_info      = ls_job_output_info
            job_output_options   = ls_job_output_options
          TABLES
            it_outtab            = gt_forms1.
      ENDLOOP.

*    WHEN ra_movo." 외주 자재이동
*      IF pv_ucomm EQ 'ZLABEL'. "이동라벨
*        lt_mov[] = gt_mov[].
*        DELETE lt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ space.
*        SORT lt_mov BY rsnum.
*        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_mov COMPARING rsnum.
*
*        LOOP AT lt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
*          _cls gt_forms1.
*          CLEAR gs_head.
*          LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x
*                             AND rsnum EQ lt_mov-rsnum.
*
*            CLEAR gt_forms1.
*
*            gt_forms1-rsnum = |{ gt_mov-rsnum ALPHA = IN }|.
*            gt_forms1-rspos = |{ gt_mov-rspos ALPHA = IN }|.
*
*            "플랜트/플랜트명
*            SELECT SINGLE name1 INTO @lv_name1
*              FROM t001w
*             WHERE werks EQ @gt_mov-werks.
*
*            gt_forms1-plant = gt_mov-werks && ` - ` && lv_name1.
*
*            gt_forms1-barcode = gt_mov-rsnum.
*            "2021.05.31
*            "begin
*            "제품명/ 제조번호
**            SELECT SINGLE MT~MAKTX
**                            M1~LICHA
**              INTO (GT_FORMS1-MAKTX, GT_FORMS1-CHARG)
**              FROM RESB AS RS INNER JOIN AFPO AS AP
**                                        ON RS~ZAUFNR EQ AP~AUFNR
**                               INNER JOIN MAKT AS MT
**                                        ON AP~MATNR EQ MT~MATNR
**                                       AND MT~SPRAS EQ SY-LANGU
**                               INNER JOIN MCH1 AS M1

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form OPEN_SMARTFORMS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM open_smartforms .
  CLEAR gs_control_parameters.
*  GS_CONTROL_PARAMETERS-NO_DIALOG = 'X'.
  gs_control_parameters-no_open   = 'X'.
  gs_control_parameters-no_close  = 'X'.
  gs_control_parameters-preview   = 'X'.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_OPEN'
    EXPORTING
      control_parameters = gs_control_parameters
    EXCEPTIONS
      formatting_error   = 1
      internal_error     = 2
      send_error         = 3
      user_canceled      = 4
      OTHERS             = 5.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form CLOSE_SMARTFORMS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM close_smartforms .
  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_CLOSE'
    EXCEPTIONS
      formatting_error = 1
      internal_error   = 2
      send_error       = 3
      OTHERS           = 4.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form PRINT_LABEL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM print_label .
  DATA : lt_field  LIKE TABLE OF sval WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_return.
  DATA lv_chk(1).

*  CASE gc_x.
*    WHEN ra_inp.    "생산투입
*      "기존 선택값 해제
*      CLEAR gt_inp-sel_flag.
*      MODIFY gt_inp TRANSPORTING sel_flag
*                        WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
*
*
*      "선택값 설정
*      CLEAR gt_inp.
*      LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
*        gt_inp-sel_flag = gc_x.
*        MODIFY gt_inp INDEX gs_rows-index
*                          TRANSPORTING sel_flag.
*
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*      "선택된 데이터 중에 자재문서번호가 하나라도 없으면 에러메세지 출력
*      READ TABLE gt_inp WITH KEY sel_flag = gc_x
*                                    mblnr = ''.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m10 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
*        EXIT.
*      ENDIF.
*
*
*    WHEN ra_inpc. "생산투입취소    2021.05.21 추가
*      "기존 선택값 해제
*      CLEAR gt_inpc-sel_flag.
*      MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING sel_flag
*                        WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
*
*
*      "선택값 설정
*      CLEAR gt_inpc.
*      LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
*        gt_inpc-sel_flag = gc_x.
*        MODIFY gt_inpc INDEX gs_rows-index
*                          TRANSPORTING sel_flag.
*
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*      "선택된 데이터 중에 자재문서번호가 하나라도 없으면 에러메세지 출력
*      READ TABLE gt_inpc WITH KEY sel_flag = gc_x
*                                    mblnr = ''.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m10 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
*        EXIT.
*      ENDIF.
*  ENDCASE.


  _cls lt_field.
  "칭량일자
  lt_field-tabname = 'MSEG'.
  lt_field-fieldname = 'BUDAT_MKPF'.
  lt_field-fieldtext = '칭량일자'.
  lt_field-value = ''.
  APPEND lt_field. CLEAR lt_field.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      popup_title     = '칭량일자를 입력하세요'
      start_column    = '50'
      start_row       = '10'
    IMPORTING
      returncode      = lv_return
    TABLES
      fields          = lt_field
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_fields = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0 OR lv_return = 'A' OR lt_field-value IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s008.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.


  CLEAR gv_budat.
  READ TABLE lt_field INDEX 1.
  gv_budat = lt_field-value.

  CHECK gv_budat IS NOT INITIAL.

  PERFORM  OPEN_SMARTFORMS.

  PERFORM  CALL_SMARTFORMS2.

  PERFORM  CLOSE_SMARTFORMS.


ENDFORM.

*& Form CALL_SMARTFORMS2

FORM call_smartforms2 .
* Internal table declaration
  DATA: lt_docs      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF docs,

*  CASE gc_x.
*    WHEN ra_inp.   "생산투입

*        gs_forms2-maktx = gs_inp-maktx.          "제품명
*
*        SELECT SINGLE licha INTO gs_forms2-licha  "제조번호
*          FROM mch1
*         WHERE matnr EQ gs_inp-plnbez
*           AND charg EQ gs_inp-charg.
*
*        gs_forms2-matnr_t = gs_inp-matnr_t.                 "원료명
*        gs_forms2-matnr  =  |{ gs_inp-matnr ALPHA = OUT }|. "원료 코드

*
*        WRITE lv_erfmg TO gs_forms2-erfmg LEFT-JUSTIFIED UNIT gs_inp-erfme.
*
*        gs_forms2-erfmg = gs_forms2-erfmg && ` ` && gs_inp-erfme.
*
*        CONCATENATE gv_budat(4) gv_budat+4(2) gv_budat+6(2)
*               INTO gs_forms2-zdate SEPARATED BY '.'. " 칭량일
*        gs_forms2-zlot = gs_inp-zlot.    " 자재배치
*        gs_forms2-barcode = gs_forms2-matnr && gs_forms2-zlot.
*
*        lv_formname = 'ZSFPP_0003'.
*
*        "===================================================================
**호출할 스마트폼 명
*

*            formname = lv_formname
*          IMPORTING
*            fm_name  = lv_fm_name.

**    LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS-TDDEST        = 'ZSAPGUI'. " 'LP01'.

*        ls_output_options-tdlifetime    = '0'.       " '0' days after test
*        ls_output_options-tdcover       = ' '.       " no cover sheet
*        ls_output_options-tdcopies      = '001'.
*
*        CLEAR: ls_document_output_info,
*             ls_job_output_info,
*             ls_job_output_options.
*
*        CALL FUNCTION lv_fm_name
*          EXPORTING

*            user_setting         = lv_user

*          IMPORTING

*            job_output_info      = ls_job_output_info
*            job_output_options   = ls_job_output_options.
*
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*    WHEN ra_inpc. " 2021.05.21 생산투입취소
*      LOOP AT gt_inpc INTO gs_inpc WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
**                                     AND MBLNR_C IS NOT INITIAL.

*
*        CLEAR gs_forms2.
*
*        gs_forms2-maktx = gs_inpc-maktx.          "제품명

*
*        lv_erfmg = gs_inpc-menge.
*

*
*        gs_forms2-erfmg = gs_forms2-erfmg && ` ` && gs_inpc-erfme.
*
*        CONCATENATE gv_budat(4) gv_budat+4(2) gv_budat+6(2)
*               INTO gs_forms2-zdate SEPARATED BY '.'. " 칭량일
*        gs_forms2-zlot = gs_inpc-zlot.    " 배치
*        gs_forms2-barcode = gs_forms2-matnr && gs_forms2-zlot.
*
*        lv_formname = 'ZSFPP_0003'.
*
*        "===================================================================
**호출할 스마트폼 명
*
*        CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
*          EXPORTING
*            formname = lv_formname

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GET_OLD_BATCH_CLASSFICATION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> <LS_INP>_WERKS
*&      --> <LS_INP>_MATNR
*&      --> <LS_INP>_ZLOT
*&      <-- <LS_INP>_AUSP1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_old_batch_classfication  USING   pv_werks
                                           pv_matnr
                                           pv_charg
                                  CHANGING pv_ausp1.

  "SD_BATCH_CLASSIFICATION_DATA function 참조

  DATA:                                "Internal Tables of Classes found
    BEGIN OF i_class OCCURS 5.
*     INCLUDE STRUCTURE CLASS.                               "v_n_516772
      include STRUCTURE sclass. "^_n_516772
  DATA:
    END OF i_class.


  DATA:
    ch_object LIKE ausp-objek,         "Object Key for Classification
    BEGIN OF mch1_object,              "Object Key MCH1
      matnr LIKE  mch1-matnr,
      charg LIKE  mch1-charg,
    END   OF mch1_object,
    ch_classtype LIKE klah-klart,      "classtype batch
    ch_table     LIKE tcla-obtab.      "object table batch

  DATA:
    BEGIN OF i_clobjdat OCCURS 50.
      INCLUDE STRUCTURE clobjdat.
  DATA:
    END   OF i_clobjdat.


  ch_classtype = '023'.
  ch_table     = 'MCH1      '.
  mch1_object-matnr = pv_matnr.  "Concatenate Object Key
  mch1_object-charg = pv_charg.  " ..
  MOVE mch1_object TO ch_object. " ..

  CALL FUNCTION 'CLAF_CLASSIFICATION_OF_OBJECTS'
    EXPORTING
      classtype         = ch_classtype
      features          = 'X'
      object            = ch_object
      objecttable       = ch_table
      initial_charact   = ''
    TABLES
      t_class           = i_class
      t_objectdata      = i_clobjdat
    EXCEPTIONS
      no_classification = 1
      no_classtypes     = 2.
  CHECK  sy-subrc  = 0.

  READ TABLE i_clobjdat WITH KEY atnam = 'ZBATCH_OLD'.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    pv_ausp1 = i_clobjdat-ausp1.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form BAPI_RESERVATION_DELETE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM bapi_reservation_delete .
  DATA lv_rsnum             TYPE bapi2093_res_key-reserv_no.
  DATA lt_item  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi2093_res_item_change.
  DATA ls_item  TYPE bapi2093_res_item_change.
  DATA lt_itemx TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapi2093_res_item_changex.
  DATA ls_itemx TYPE bapi2093_res_item_changex.
  DATA lt_new   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF  bapi2093_res_item_new.
  DATA ls_new   TYPE bapi2093_res_item_new.

*  DATA ls_zmms0070 TYPE zmms0070.
  DATA lv_rspos LIKE resb-rspos.
  DATA lt_ext_res TYPE bapiparex_tab.
  DATA lt_extension          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiparex.
  DATA ls_extension           TYPE bapiparex.

  DATA lv_cnt TYPE i.

  DATA lt_return               TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2.


  DATA: BEGIN OF lt_head OCCURS 0,
          rsnum LIKE resb-rsnum,
          rspos LIKE resb-rspos,
        END OF lt_head.

  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_mov-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.

  "선택값 설정
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    CLEAR gt_mov.
    gt_mov-sel_flag = gc_x.

    MODIFY gt_mov INDEX gs_rows-index
                   TRANSPORTING sel_flag.
  ENDLOOP.

  _cls lt_head.
  LOOP AT gt_mov WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
    MOVE gt_mov-rsnum TO lt_head-rsnum.
    MOVE gt_mov-rspos TO lt_head-rspos.
    _app lt_head.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT lt_head BY rsnum rspos.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_head COMPARING rsnum rspos.

  LOOP AT lt_head.
*    "Reservation Number
    lv_rsnum = lt_head-rsnum.

    CLEAR lv_cnt.
    _cls: lt_item, lt_new.

    LOOP AT  gt_mov WHERE rsnum = lt_head-rsnum
                       AND rspos = lt_head-rspos
                       AND sel_flag = gc_x.
      CLEAR ls_item.
      ls_item-res_item = gt_mov-rspos.
      ls_itemx-res_item = gt_mov-rspos.

      ls_item-delete_ind = gc_x.
      ls_itemx-delete_ind = gc_x.

      APPEND ls_item TO lt_item.
      APPEND ls_itemx TO lt_itemx.
    ENDLOOP.


    REFRESH lt_return.
    IF lt_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_RESERVATION_CHANGE'
        EXPORTING
          reservation               = lv_rsnum
*         TESTRUN                   = TESTRUN
*         ATPCHECK                  = ATPCHECK
        TABLES
          reservationitems_changed  = lt_item
          reservationitems_changedx = lt_itemx
*         RESERVATIONITEMS_NEW      = LT_NEW
          return                    = lt_return.

      CLEAR gt_mov.
      READ TABLE lt_return INTO DATA(ls_return) INDEX lines( lt_return ).
      IF ls_return-type EQ gc_e.
        CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
        gt_mov-icon = gc_err.
        gt_mov-msg = ls_return-message.

      ELSE.
        CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
          EXPORTING
            wait = gc_x.
        gt_mov-icon = gc_suc.
        gt_mov-msg = TEXT-m03.
      ENDIF.

      LOOP AT lt_item INTO ls_item.

        MODIFY gt_mov TRANSPORTING icon msg
                       WHERE rsnum EQ lt_head-rsnum
                         AND rspos EQ lt_head-rspos
                         AND sel_flag EQ gc_x.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

*& Form INI_ALV2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM ini_alv2 .
  IF go_cust_container IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT go_cust_container
      EXPORTING
        container_name = 'CC_CON'.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM  DEFINE_GRID_POPUP.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form DEFINE_GRID_POPUP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM define_grid_popup .
  DATA: lt_fcat TYPE lvc_t_fcat.
  DATA: lt_excl_func TYPE ui_functions,
        ls_layo      TYPE lvc_s_layo.

  IF go_grid_0300 IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT go_grid_0300
      EXPORTING
        i_parent = go_cust_container.

    PERFORM  GRID_SET_TOOLBAR CHANGING lt_excl_func .
    PERFORM  GRID_SET_LAYO CHANGING ls_layo.
    PERFORM  SET_FIELDCATALOG CHANGING lt_fcat.

    PERFORM  GRID_EVENT_HANDLER_REGISTER USING go_grid_0300 'OBJ8'.

    CALL METHOD go_grid_0300->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = ls_layo
        it_toolbar_excluding = lt_excl_func
      CHANGING
        it_outtab            = gt_split[]
        it_fieldcatalog      = lt_fcat[].
  ELSE.

    PERFORM  GRID_SET_TOOLBAR CHANGING gt_excl_func .
    PERFORM  GRID_SET_LAYO CHANGING gs_layout_alv .
    PERFORM  SET_FIELDCATALOG CHANGING lt_fcat.

    CALL METHOD go_grid_0300->set_table_for_first_display
      EXPORTING
        is_layout            = ls_layo
        it_toolbar_excluding = lt_excl_func
      CHANGING
        it_outtab            = gt_split[]
        it_fieldcatalog      = lt_fcat[].

    CALL METHOD cl_gui_control=>set_focus
      EXPORTING
        control = go_grid_0300.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form SET_FIELDCATALOG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      <-- LT_FCAT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM set_fieldcatalog  CHANGING pt_fcat TYPE lvc_t_fcat.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <ls_fcat> TYPE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: lv_date TYPE sydatum.

  lv_date = sy-datum + 1.
  SET PARAMETER ID 'ALVBUFFER' FIELD lv_date.
  CLEAR:   pt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.
  REFRESH: pt_fcat, gt_slis_fcat.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
      i_program_name     = sy-repid
      i_internal_tabname = 'GT_SPLIT'
      i_inclname         = sy-repid
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat        = gt_slis_fcat[].

* SLIS로 만든 필드 구조를 CLASS ALV 구조로 생성

  CALL FUNCTION 'LVC_TRANSFER_FROM_SLIS'
    EXPORTING
      it_fieldcat_alv = gt_slis_fcat
    IMPORTING
      et_fieldcat_lvc = pt_fcat[]
    TABLES
      it_data         = gt_split
    EXCEPTIONS
      it_data_missing = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF  sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE  ID sy-msgid  TYPE sy-msgty  NUMBER sy-msgno
             WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

* Field Catalog Modify
  LOOP AT pt_fcat ASSIGNING <ls_fcat>.
    CASE <ls_fcat>-fieldname.
      WHEN 'ZNO'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = 'No.'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'BDMNG'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '수량'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-qfieldname = 'ERFME'.
      WHEN 'WGT1'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '완포'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-edit = gc_x.
        <ls_fcat>-qfieldname = 'ERFME'.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_color_total.
      WHEN 'PRT1'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '인쇄매수'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-edit = gc_x.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_color_total.
      WHEN 'WGT2'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '잔량'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-edit = gc_x.
        <ls_fcat>-qfieldname = 'ERFME'.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_color_total.
      WHEN 'PRT2'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '인쇄매수'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-edit = gc_x.
        <ls_fcat>-style = alv_style_color_total.
      WHEN 'TOT'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '총합계'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN 'CHARG'.
        <ls_fcat>-coltext = '배치'.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_l = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_m = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
        <ls_fcat>-scrtext_s = <ls_fcat>-coltext.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        <ls_fcat>-tech = gc_x.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form SELECT_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_data .
  DATA:BEGIN OF lt_ord OCCURS 0,
         aufnr LIKE afko-aufnr,
         matnr LIKE mara-matnr,
       END OF lt_ord.

  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_inp-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_inp TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_inp.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_inp-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_inp INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  "자재배치값이 없는 경우 에러메세지 출력
  READ TABLE gt_inp WITH KEY sel_flag = gc_x
                                zlot = ''.

  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m14 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  "생산오더/구성품이 하나이상 선택시에는 에러메세지 출력
  _cls lt_ord.
  LOOP AT gt_inp WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
    MOVE gt_inp-aufnr TO lt_ord-aufnr.
    MOVE gt_inp-matnr TO lt_ord-matnr.
    APPEND lt_ord.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT lt_ord BY aufnr matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ord COMPARING aufnr matnr.

  IF lines( lt_ord ) > 1.
    MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m12 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: gv_date, gv_split.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form DATA_CHANGED_FINISHED2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> E_MODIFIED
*&      --> ET_GOOD_CELLS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM data_changed_finished2   USING pe_modified
                                    pe_endmethod TYPE lvc_t_modi.

  DATA ls_endmethod TYPE LINE OF lvc_t_modi.
  DATA l_stbl TYPE lvc_s_stbl.
  DATA ls_split LIKE LINE OF gt_split.
  DATA lv_cnt TYPE i.

  READ TABLE pe_endmethod INTO ls_endmethod INDEX 1.

  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
  CLEAR ls_split.
  READ TABLE gt_split INTO ls_split INDEX ls_endmethod-row_id.
  CHECK ls_split IS NOT INITIAL.
  "완포/잔량/인쇄매수 입력마다 합계 산출
  ls_split-tot = ( ls_split-wgt1 * ls_split-prt1 ) +
                 ( ls_split-wgt2 * ls_split-prt2 ).

  MODIFY gt_split FROM ls_split INDEX ls_endmethod-row_id.

  l_stbl-row = gc_x.
  l_stbl-col = gc_x.
  CALL METHOD go_grid_0300->refresh_table_display
    EXPORTING
      is_stable      = l_stbl
      i_soft_refresh = gc_x.

ENDFORM.

                                              matnr = gt_split-matnr
                                              zlot = gt_split-charg
                                              sel_flag = gc_x.

    CHECK sy-subrc EQ 0.
    DO gt_split-prt1 TIMES.
      CLEAR gs_forms2.

      gs_forms2-maktx = gs_inp-maktx.          "제품명

        FROM mch1

         AND charg EQ gs_inp-charg.

      gs_forms2-matnr  =  |{ gs_inp-matnr ALPHA = OUT }|. "원료 코드

      CLEAR lv_erfmg.
      lv_erfmg = gt_split-wgt1.

      WRITE lv_erfmg TO gs_forms2-erfmg LEFT-JUSTIFIED UNIT gs_inp-erfme.

      gs_forms2-erfmg = gs_forms2-erfmg && ` ` && gs_inp-erfme.

      CONCATENATE gv_date(4) gv_date+4(2) gv_date+6(2)
             INTO gs_forms2-zdate SEPARATED BY '.'. " 칭량일
      gs_forms2-zlot = gs_inp-zlot.    " 자재배치
      gs_forms2-barcode = gs_forms2-matnr && gs_forms2-zlot.

        EXPORTING
          formname = lv_formname
        IMPORTING
          fm_name  = lv_fm_name.

*    LS_OUTPUT_OPTIONS-TDDEST        = 'ZSAPGUI'. " 'LP01'.
      ls_output_options-tdarmod       = 'X'.

      ls_output_options-tdlifetime    = '0'.       " '0' days after test
      ls_output_options-tdcover       = ' '.       " no cover sheet
      ls_output_options-tdcopies      = '001'.

           ls_job_output_info,
           ls_job_output_options.

          job_output_info      = ls_job_output_info

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form SELECT_DATA2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM select_data2 .
  DATA:BEGIN OF lt_ord OCCURS 0,
         aufnr LIKE afko-aufnr,
         matnr LIKE mara-matnr,
       END OF lt_ord.

  "기존 선택값 해제
  CLEAR gt_inpc-sel_flag.
  MODIFY gt_inpc TRANSPORTING sel_flag
                    WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.


  "선택값 설정
  CLEAR gt_inpc.
  LOOP AT gt_rows INTO gs_rows.
    gt_inpc-sel_flag = gc_x.
    MODIFY gt_inpc INDEX gs_rows-index
                      TRANSPORTING sel_flag.

  ENDLOOP.

  "자재배치값이 없는 경우 에러메세지 출력
  READ TABLE gt_inpc WITH KEY sel_flag = gc_x
                                zlot = ''.

  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m14 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  "생산오더/구성품이 하나이상 선택시에는 에러메세지 출력
  _cls lt_ord.
  LOOP AT gt_inpc WHERE sel_flag EQ gc_x.
    MOVE gt_inpc-aufnr TO lt_ord-aufnr.
    MOVE gt_inpc-matnr TO lt_ord-matnr.
    APPEND lt_ord.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT lt_ord BY aufnr matnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ord COMPARING aufnr matnr.

  IF lines( lt_ord ) > 1.
    MESSAGE s000 WITH TEXT-m12 DISPLAY LIKE gc_e.
    gv_answer = gc_n.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: gv_date, gv_split.
  _cls gt_split.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F4_VARIANT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      <-- P_VAR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f4_variant  CHANGING p_variant.

  DATA: ls_variant TYPE disvariant,
        l_exit     TYPE char1.

  ls_variant-report = sy-repid.
  CASE gc_x.
    WHEN ra_req .
      ls_variant-handle = '1'.
    WHEN ra_chg .
      ls_variant-handle = '2'.
    WHEN ra_mov. "OR ra_movo.
      ls_variant-handle = '3'.
    WHEN ra_dis .
      ls_variant-handle = '4'.
*    WHEN ra_inp.
*      ls_variant-handle = '5'.
*    WHEN ra_inpc.
*      ls_variant-handle = '6'.
  ENDCASE.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_VARIANT_F4'
    EXPORTING
      is_variant = ls_variant
      i_save     = 'A'
*     it_default_fieldcat =
    IMPORTING
      e_exit     = l_exit
      es_variant = ls_variant
    EXCEPTIONS
      not_found  = 2.
  IF sy-subrc = 2.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'S' NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ELSE.
    IF l_exit EQ space.
      p_variant = ls_variant-variant.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
